  - A run whose machine takes AUTH with Sign-in on Auto, not a dry run, in the top frame, adds `sign-in-auto` and, once it rests paused, calls `RunEnv.signIn` (the runtime's `signIn` of `content/sign-in.ts`): the form (the adapter's `signInForm`, else `standardSignInForm`: autocomplete `current-password` or the one visible password box for a sign-in; `new-password` boxes, one or two, for a new account; the email box by `username`/`email`, else `type=email`; one form, one visible submit control; its required, unticked boxes) and `signInRefusal` (https; each box connected, visible, enabled, editable and no honeypot: `tabindex=-1`, under `aria-hidden`, smaller than 8 px, or camouflaged; one form; its `action` and the button's `formaction`, when written, resolved as the browser does, through `HTMLFormElement.prototype`'s and the control's prototype's getters, against the document's base address, on the page's own origin; its `method`, and a written `formmethod`, POST (`method` otherwise); a page that throws while it is read is refused, never filled) before anything is asked; then `signin/credential {wall}`; then, with nothing awaited, the address checked again (same origin, https), the sign-in's host held to the page's (`host` equal, or `*` with `signInHostCovered`), the form read and refused again (the same password box), the boxes written (`setValue`, input, change, leaving the box: `dom/fillers/events.ts`) and read back, the terms clicked (`clickIfAllowed`), and the button pressed by `clickAdapterSignIn` (lint: `SIGN_IN_PRESS_RULE`, `sign-in.ts` alone), which finds it itself and refuses it near the application form's Submit, Next or never-pressed buttons. The outcome, watched every 250 ms for `SIGN_IN_SETTLE_MS` (20 s): the document leaving ('left'), the password box gone or an email code or CAPTCHA shown ('continue': `RunEnv.afterSignIn`, the runtime's Continue with the run's own trigger, which scans again), an error shown with the box still there or nothing within the wait ('failed'). 'failed' is `sign-in-failed`, a refusal or none `sign-in-unavailable`, each reported again.
  - The worker (`background/sign-in.ts`) answers `signin/credential` only to a top frame (`frameId` 0), outside a private tab, at an https address (Chrome's sender address), while Sign-in acts as Auto, once per tab, wall and host in `SIGN_IN_ONCE_MS` (10 min; `storage.session` `signin-tried:<tab>|<wall>|<the first 16 hex digits of the host's SHA-256>`: a time, set only once TailorIT answered a sign-in, so TailorIT down or none saved answers `unavailable` and may be asked again): GET /credentials?host= (`server-client.ts` `credential`: the host checked as a host name, in the query string), then the answer's host held to the sender's. It answers `{host, email, password}` to that frame (`SignInCredentialAnswerSchema`), the only message that carries it; the content script holds it in a local of `signIn` until it is typed.
- **Messages added:** content → worker `signin/credential {wall}`; `run/begin`'s trigger `agent`; page → worker `agent/status`, `agent/set {on, confirmed?}`, `agent/stop` (each answering `AgentStatus`); worker → content `run/start {dryRun, agent?, retry?}` and `agent/enter` (`EntryAnswerSchema {outcome, block?}`), both from the worker's script alone; `panel/changed {what: 'agent'}`; the notices `sign-in-auto`, `sign-in-unavailable`, `sign-in-failed`.
- **The disclosure** is version 3 (`DISCLOSURE_VERSION`; 4 since v3 Phase 7a, which names Workable among the sites; 5 since v3 Phases 6 and 7b, which name Workday's, iCIMS's, Taleo's, SuccessFactors' and Oracle's pages and Sign-in Auto's email-first step; 6 since v3 Phase 8, the company career sites; 7 since the snapshot build of 2026-11-06: SmartRecruiters and fifteen more ATSs, Oracle's government cloud and jobs.workable.com, the job pages alone on hosts that also serve employees, the page's path sent for the sign-in on ADP's and ClearCompany's candidate pages, the tab's chain records, and the fill beside an unticked checkbox CAPTCHA, every press waiting for the person): until the person acknowledged it, Submit and Sign-in stay Manual and Run auto-apply cannot be turned on (an agent left on is blocked: `disclosure`).
- **Tests:** `background/agent.test.ts` (over a queue fake that keeps TailorIT's rules: the verdicts, claim and resume after a restart, another browser session's record, the canonical address, the task's own application, one Submit per job, the start cap, the retry, the backoff, the results kept and dropped, stop, blocked, the connection changed, each stop reason, the progress timeout), `background/agent-worker.test.ts` (the wiring, the agent's settings and run/begin, Submit held in another document, the task's job and resume in the lookup, the result instead of /applied, the tab released then closed, the switch off at a disconnect, the credential's rules and its marker), `content/sign-in.browser.test.ts` (the forms, the honeypots, http, another origin, a `<base>` elsewhere, shadowing controls, GET and dialog, another host, the run's sign-in, a Cancel before the password arrives, the agent's messages from the worker alone, the entry's blocks and its sign-in, a never-pressed entry, the RunState resumed across documents), `test/allow-submit.test.ts` (the doors and their wrappers confined), `test/mock-server.test.ts` (the mock's queue rules), `e2e/specs/38-agent.spec.ts` (the agent over the mock's queue on synthetic Greenhouse, Lever (its /apply sending it to the posting first) and Ashby pages; a CAPTCHA and a no-AI posting; Stop; Sign-in Auto and Manual on the synthetic sign-in and create-account pages; TailorIT's own pages and a job site's script reaching nothing). The e2e browser resolves no host name and asks no proxy (`e2e/fixtures.ts` `noNetwork`: the e2e build's localhost is the mock's 127.0.0.1, on which alone the mock listens; the production build's localhost, the owner's TailorIT at 3100, resolves to nothing, as `00-smoke` proves), and the e2e build lets the agent's new window rest 750 ms on its empty page before the task's address opens in it, so Playwright routes it first: no request of the suite can reach a real site. A routed HTTP redirect is never used: Chrome follows one without routing its new request (a script's `location.replace` is routed).

## Autofill API version 3 (the v3 pull, 2026-11-02)
TailorIT v1.2 `70706b2` speaks autofill API version 3 (its docs/ARCHITECTURE.md, "Jobright parity, TailorIT's side"), and an extension that sends 2 gets 409 on every token request. The extension was moved to 3 with no new feature (docs/CONTRACT.md lists the routes and what each one is for):
- **The pull** (`npm run contract:pull -- F:\Develop\TailorIT`): `src/contract/tailorit/` is TailorIT's contract at `70706b2`, so `AUTOFILL_API_VERSION` (the header every request sends, and the one an answer's header is compared with) is 3. The copy is never edited; `test/contract.test.ts` checks it names version 3.
- **What the extension parses now and does not use yet** (v3 Phases 3b and 4 use the version 3 routes since: the section above): the scopes `profile:write`, `tracker:write`, `agent:run` and `credentials:read` (kept with the token in IndexedDB and in `auth/status`, both of whose schemas take the contract's scope list; the options page still says only whether `profile:sensitive` is held), a lookup's `matchedBy: 'chosen'`, the profile's `anythingElse` note and the envelopes' `anythingElseFlaggedLines` (parsed with the profile and held in memory with it, never read by the canonical profile, the plan, a log line or a message), and a draft question's optional `instruction` (never sent). The golden profile (`fixtures/profiles/alex-rivera.profile.json`) carries `anythingElse: ''`, as GET /profile now answers it.
- **What changed by itself:** the contract's `parseAtsUrl` reads Ashby's addresses (the board in lower case), so `addablePosting` takes an Ashby posting: the job card offers Add this job to TailorIT on an Ashby posting TailorIT does not have, as on Greenhouse and Lever (TailorIT adds Ashby's since version 3), and the lookup on load sends an Ashby posting's canonical address as TailorIT stores it (`jobs.ashbyhq.com/<board in lower case>/<id>`, `lookupAddressOf`). `src/adapters/url.test.ts` lists where the adapter's reading and TailorIT's differ (the board's spelling, and a board page's `?ashby_jid=`).
- **Pairing again:** a token made before version 3 still does what it did; TailorIT refuses it the version 3 routes (403, "Connect the extension again"), which come with the next features. So the person connects once more after the update. TailorIT's connect dialog now has two boxes: "Let this browser read and change work authorization and self-identification answers" (ticked by default) and "Let this browser sign in to job sites with my saved password" (`allowSignIn`, unticked by default; it grants `credentials:read`, which only the later Sign-in mode Auto would use).

## v3 Phase 7a: Workable (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12, Decision 11)
Workable's application host, `apply.workable.com`, joins the ATS list (`src/shared/hosts.ts` `ATS_MATCHES`, so the manifest's matches and host permissions, the bridge's matches and check-build follow), with its own adapter (`src/adapters/workable/`), written from the read-only capture of 2026-11-02 (docs/FIXTURES.md "The Workable capture": 7 closed forms, 8 states and the 120 forms' question lists). SmartRecruiters' form is behind DataDome and waits for the owner's snapshots.
- **Addresses** (`workable/url.ts`): `/<account>/j/<SHORTCODE>/apply/` is the form and `/<account>/j/<SHORTCODE>/` the overview, with or without the slash, the account folded to lower case and the 10-hex shortcode to upper case, the query and fragment dropped: one application key for every spelling. `/j/<code>`, `<account>.workable.com/j/...` and `www.workable.com/j/...` only redirect (301) and are not read; `jobs.workable.com` (Workable's job search) is not matched. Registered in `PLATFORM_ADAPTERS`, `URL_PARSERS` and `company.ts`.
- **In TailorIT's contract since its API 4** (`AUTOFILL_API_VERSION` 4, TailorIT v1.2 `a597049`): `ATS_KINDS` ends with `workable`, equal to `PLATFORMS` (`test/contract.test.ts`); a Workable field's AI classification is asked under platform `workable`, a saved answer keeps platform `workable` and a free-text one the scope `workable:<account>` (`companyOfUrl`), the lookup reads the posting from its address (`parseAtsUrl`), Add job is offered where the address names the account (`addablePosting`), and the agent opens a queued Workable job at `apply.workable.com/<account>/j/<SHORTCODE>/apply/` (`agentApplicationUrl`). The two sides read two addresses apart (`src/adapters/url.test.ts`): a `/j/<shortcode>` link naming no account and the account's own host, which TailorIT reads and the extension, which runs on `apply.workable.com` alone, does not. Until this version the extension filled Workable without naming it to TailorIT (`PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT`, now gone).
- **The adapter** (`workable/index.ts`, every hook naming its fixture): the root is `form[data-ui=application-form]` once it holds its fields, drawn 4.6 to 7.5 s after the page loads (`quirks.formLateMs`, 30 s); the "Autofill application" box (`[data-ui=autofill-button]`'s "Import resume from") is `excluded`; questions are labelled by their title row's `span[id$=_label]` (`labelFor`), the phone's country button by the phone's title with "country code" (`labelSuffix`), the consent box by the text its `role=checkbox` names; required is the title row's "*" (`requiredFor`), and always for the GDPR consent, which Workable refuses an application without (its form API: `required: true, onlyTrueAllowed: true`) though the page marks nothing; help text is the field's `[id$=_helper]`; sections are each `section[data-ui=section]`'s `h2[id$=_section_name]` (the GDPR consent's, with none, as the section before it) and the Education and Experience blocks; `checkedOf` reads the `role=radio`/`role=checkbox` element's `aria-checked`, which Workable sets at once (the input's own `checked` one render later: the probe of 2026-11-02); `formatFor` gives each date mask the layout its placeholder spells in day, month and year tokens (MM/DD/YYYY, MM/YYYY, and an account's DD/MM/YYYY or YYYY-MM-DD: the review) and the number box (no id, its `data-ui` its name) 'number', read back as a number ("85000" shows "85,000"); `postingText` is the overview's description, requirements and benefits; `entryControl` the overview's "Apply for this job" or APPLICATION tab (both never pressed by a run: `entryNeverPressed`); Submit is `button[data-ui=apply-button][type=submit]`, one step, so final; after Submit `[data-ui=successful-submit]` is the sign (`workableSubmitted`, Jobright 1.25.2's selector, not seen live; `content/submitted.ts`).
- **Widgets:** a dropdown (`div[data-input-type=select] input[role=combobox][readonly=true]`) is declared with `readOnlyBox` (its readonly box does not make the field read-only), opened by `click()` on its box, its list read in place (a non-modal dialog inside the field, named by `aria-controls`), its option's label `[data-role=option-label]`, `highlightIsNoChoice`, and closed by the declared Escape sent to the list itself (`keyTarget: 'list'`: focus moves there as it opens, and focusout, blur and a second click leave it open); Workable draws that list `position: fixed` at the box's place on screen, so a dropdown below the fold opens its list beyond the viewport, where no fixed element counts as seen: the declaration says so (`listFollowsField`), and the list's fixed box is judged by the off-screen rule as a page element below the fold is, its field's way (`dom/visibility.ts` `followsItsField`, only for a fixed box inside the field's own part of the page and drawn at the field: its top or bottom within the field's height and 24 px, across the field's width; the mark goes when the run is done with the list, `stopsFollowing` in `closeList`; every other rule stands, and every other fixed box is judged as before; the live check of 2026-11-02 found the lists unread and left open without it); the declared Escape is checked by `assertKeyTarget` (`fillers/not-submit.ts`), which reads the list's own words, never its options' (an option "I will submit my portfolio later" no longer refuses the key); the phone's country (intl-tel-input's `button.iti__selected-country`, a `widgetRoots` candidate) is a select-only combobox of 244 countries ("Canada (+1)"), its answer read from the button's title and dial code (`shownAnswer`), closed with Escape on its list. Yes/No, radios and checkbox groups keep the label proxy and a `click()` on their hidden inputs (never `.checked`, never a Yes/No's option div).
- **Dates:** a Workable date mask takes a written value only after a keydown, and then opens its calendar; Enter picks today. `Adapter.keyAroundWrite` names Escape for its boxes, and the text filler sends it (a keydown and a keyup, through `assertNotSubmit`) right before the value is set and once after the events, which closes the calendar (`fillers/text.ts`). The box's `inputmode="tel"` is a keypad, not a layout: an adapter's `formatFor` now takes the place of a format that came from inputmode alone (`scan.ts` `withAdapterFormat`), the placeholder's pattern standing.
- **Labels the engine reads differently, for every adapter that declares the hook:** an adapter's upload control never labels its own input (`labels.ts` `uploadControlsAmong`: Workable's "Choose file" is a `label[for]` that the input's `aria-labelledby` also names, so Resume and Photo were both "Choose file"); and an adapter's label inside the control's own wrapping label, where the two read differently, is the label (Workable's number and date boxes, whose `<label>` holds the star before the question, and its consent box in two nested labels, whose texts would join twice). Lever's resume question, whose wrapping label reads as its adapter's label, is unchanged.
- **Education and Experience** (`repeatables`): blocks are `[data-ui=<kind>] > ul > li` (a saved entry's summary or the open editor), Add is the section's "+ Add", which Workable disables while an editor is open, `currentlyHere` the editor's `input[name=current]`, and `save` its "Update" (`data-ui=save-section`): until it is pressed the entry is not in the form. `content/repeatables.ts` `saveBlocks` presses it after the step's fill and its final Guard, never in a dry run, one `click()` through `clickIfAllowed`, settled after, only in a block the run opened (an Add of its own) or found empty when the step began, that the person edited nothing in, and whose rows are ready (`rowsReadyToSave`: each verified or skipped with nothing asked of the person, none changed by the person, one at least holding our value, written or already right); any other block is the person's (the needs-click notice). The fill step then goes round again (`orchestrator.ts` `fillStep`): add, fill and save the next entry while a section still lacks one and shows its Add (`moreToAdd`), at most as many rounds as the profile has entries; an entry still without a block is the person's (`entriesLeft`), which holds Submit mode Auto (`entries-left`, the `submit-held-entries` notice). A saved entry's summary (`dd[data-ui=school|title|company|period]`) is read by `prefill.readEntries` and sent with the plan request (`RepeatableRequest.shown`, page text as a field's value is), so the worker finds the entry it holds by its fingerprint (`core/steps/blocks.ts`; a summary takes no entry by its place), and an entry the page's draft put back is never added again. The saved blocks' rows are those their round left, never read back at rest (`savedBlocks`): the page folded their fields into its summary.
- **An "Address" that asks for a place:** Workable's Address says "Include your city, region, and country": a label that is the word "Address" alone (no street, line, flat or unit word in it) whose help text asks for a city and a country and no street, postcode or ZIP is classified as the location (`classifyRules.ts` `PLACE_HINT`, `ADDRESS_ALONE`), written "Austin, TX"; "Street address", "Address line 1" and the like stay the street, whatever their help says; the IP guess Workable prefills there is replaced and flagged (`prefill-conflict`).
- **The classifier and planner, from the capture's wordings (the review):** "Work Authorization Status" and "which best describes your work authorization status" ask which status (`workAuth.status`), never yes or no; "legally eligible to be employed" is authorization; an experience block's own "Summary" or "Description" is the role's description (`experience.description`); a slash-joined option ("Native/Fluent") matches either part at the exact stage (`slash-part`, 0.95; never in a degree or EEO domain). A question that opens "If yes", "If no", "If so", "If you answered…" or "If you selected…" is a follow-up (`core/plan/conditional.ts`): an optional one is never drafted or guessed, and a required one only once the answer above, as written now, is the one it follows; otherwise it is left (`conditional-question`). An AI answer for a number box (format number, integer or currency) is reduced to the one number it states ("$85,000" and "85k" are 85000; a range or words are `llm-invalid-output`), and a number box that drops a write reads `value-rejected-by-site`. A check that the applicant used the posting's phrase or code word is an attention check only the person answers (`PERSON_ONLY_ATTENTION`, never asked of the AI, whatever the scope), and "everything in this application is true and your own" is an AI attestation; an attention check left for the person holds Submit mode Auto (`submit-held-attention`). "In your own words" and "write it yourself" are a posting policy notice (`own-words`).
- **Never pressed** (`neverPress`): the autofill box, every section's Clear, "Choose file" and the drop zones (a file's remove control among them), a saved entry's Edit and Delete, an editor's Cancel, the cookie banner's buttons and the footer's cookie settings, the Overview and Application tabs, the company logo, "View all jobs" and "Apply for this job".
- **The browser draft and the files:** Workable saves no answer to its server before Submit (`autosaves` stays off), but keeps every answer in the page's own `localStorage` for an hour and restores it on a reload, and uploads a file to its storage the moment it goes into an upload (a POST to its S3 bucket, `workable-application-form.s3.us-east-1.amazonaws.com`, which the live check aborted, after which Workable's page showed its error page): docs/PRIVACY.md. So a pass writes its files after every other field (`quirks.filesLast`), and `uploadState` reads the page's word: failed when the form is gone or an alert by the upload names the file or its upload, confirmed when the drop zone shows the file's name with a control to remove it and no progress, pending otherwise (waited for as Ashby's, at most 10 s); a failed upload's row keeps its word when the page falls over with its field (`core/loop/rows.ts`). No upload that worked has been seen live: the confirmed sign is the synthetic page's, until the owner's snapshots of an upload that worked and one that failed.
- **Applied:** after a Submit the page confirms, the extension tells TailorIT as on any platform (POST /applied with the lookup's job, else the address); before API 4 TailorIT found no job and answered 404 (the mock still does so for an address on no ATS the contract names), and the panel said the application was not recorded.
- **An answer's layout (API 4):** an AI answer item names the layout its text must have (`AnswerItem.format`, `core/plan/answers.ts` `answerFormatOf`): a number box's `number`, `integer` or `currency`, a date input's `date` (YYYY-MM-DD, written as a date) and a text date box's `MM/DD/YYYY`, `DD/MM/YYYY`, `MM/YYYY` or `date` as its pattern shows it; a date box of any other layout, a month input and a birth date are never asked. The worker checks the answer in its layout again with the contract's `answerInFormat` (`ai-tasks.ts` `answerVerdicts`: one value of it, as TailorIT writes it, else `llm-invalid-output`), and the Guard lets an AI answer write a date input's date (`guard/provenance.ts`).
- **The live check of 2026-11-02** (the e2e build paired with the mock's dummy profile, with its AI answering every question; the labella, cloudfactory, blueground and blueground (Athens) forms; every non-GET request aborted; the resume kept out, since its upload is a POST: `resume-fails`; Dry run then Fill, never Submit): every dry run wrote nothing. The fills verified 29 of 36 rows, 15 of 23, 21 of 26 and 8 of 10: the text boxes, the phone (its country already the profile's), the Address (flagged, the IP guess replaced), Yes/No and radios, the dropdowns read and chosen below the fold (none left open), the GDPR consent ticked, one Education and one Experience entry each added, filled (MM/YYYY dates with the keys around them) and kept with Update. Left for the person: the resume (kept out), the date (no profile value), checkbox groups (no AI answer for several options), a salary in the number box (the mock's answer is no number, so the page dropped it: needs-your-click), and a dropdown whose options matched nothing. The first run, with the resume, showed the upload above; the second, before `listFollowsField`, read no dropdown below the fold and left their lists open. Screenshots and per-field rows are kept outside the repository.
- **Tests:** `src/adapters/workable/url.test.ts`, the shared table in `src/adapters/url.test.ts`; `workable.browser.test.ts` (each closed form and the editor state against its hand-written expectations, the dropdown's in-place list, the 244 countries, the states' `aria-checked`, the open calendar, Resume and Photo, the never-pressed list, the overview; and what the generic adapter made of the same pages); `workable-widgets.browser.test.ts` on `fixtures/synthetic/workable-widgets` (each widget's fill and read-back with its event log: only the declared keys, on the list or the date box; the date refused without the adapter's key; the editors saved, one left open); `e2e/specs/39-workable.spec.ts` (a late form filled end to end, both jobs and the school added, filled and saved over rounds, only Escape sent, the Address flagged prefill-conflict, the resume verified once the page kept it; a dry run; the panel's Submit to Workable's thanks, the trap invariants before and after it, the saved rows still verified, the accepted matches sealed and Applied not recorded until the contract names Workable; the overview's "do not use AI" carried by its Apply into a profile-only fill; a refused upload flagged upload-failed after every other field; a salary written "$85,000" going in as one number and a start date in MM/DD/YYYY; a restored entry matched by its summary, never added again).

## Autofill API version 5 (the API 5 pull, 2026-11-06)
TailorIT v1.2 `ee3fa32` (its v3 Phase 8 batch) speaks autofill API version 5, and an extension that sends 4 gets 409 `conflict` on every token request. `npm run contract:pull -- F:\Develop\TailorIT` copied its contract (15 files, 339 examples; read only, TailorIT untouched) and the extension speaks 5 (`AUTOFILL_API_VERSION`, the mock's `MOCK_API_VERSION`). The version adds no scope, so a connection made under 4 keeps working once the extension is reloaded: no new pairing.
- **The platforms:** `ATS_KINDS` ends with `icims`, `taleo`, `successfactors` and `oracle`, equal to `PLATFORMS` as it then stood; `PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT` was empty (v3 Phase 8 added `site` to it, and API 6, below, lists the new ATSs and SmartRecruiters). Their AI classification is asked under their name and a saved answer keeps their platform (`ai-tasks.ts`, `row-actions.ts` follow ATS_KINDS).
- **Addresses:** `parseAtsUrl` reads Workday's, iCIMS's, Taleo's, SuccessFactors' and Oracle's, and agrees with the adapters' parsers on every row of `src/adapters/url.test.ts` (platform, board and job id; no row needs a reason any more). A posting page of the five with no form is now a card frame (`frames.ts` `postingAddress`) and is looked up as it loads; Add this job is offered on a Workday posting (`addablePosting`, which refuses an unsafe location or posting segment, `unsafeWorkdaySegment`, and a path over `WORKDAY_PATH_MAX`), and on the other four it is not (TailorIT adds them only from the page: POST /api/autofill/jobs/from-page, which the extension does not call yet; the mock answers it with the contract's example).
- **Sign-in hosts:** `signInHostCovered` takes `login.icims.com`, `<company>.tbe.taleo.net` and a region-less Oracle tenant (`<tenant>.fa.oraclecloud.com`, JPMC's), so the every-site sign-in answers for them; the extension's own matches are unchanged (it runs on none of those pages but Oracle's candidate pages).
- **The pull of TailorIT `b8085f4`** (its audit of 2026-11-06, the same API version): `tracker.ts`'s `AUTOFILL_APPLIED_REFUSALS` gains `unsupported: 400` (a posting TailorIT will not read: one off its board's public list, a site whose robots.txt keeps it out). The worker reads POST /applied's refusals by their HTTP status, so a 400 is final as before: the side panel says it was not recorded, the arm goes, and Try again asks nothing (`phase3b-worker.test.ts`).
- **LinkedIn and company sites** (`parseLinkedInJobUrl`, the lookup's `employerUrl` and `page`, POST /jobs/from-page): in the contract, used by nothing here. v3 Phase 8 (below) takes the company career sites without them; LinkedIn is deferred to v5 by the owner (2026-11-06).

## Autofill API version 6 (the API 6 pull, 2026-11-06)
TailorIT v1.2 `68e64b4` speaks autofill API version 6 (its `packages/shared/src/autofill/version.ts` and its docs/ARCHITECTURE.md "version 6"). `npm run contract:pull -- F:/Develop/TailorIT` copied its contract byte for byte (15 files, 345 examples; `src/contract/tailorit/SOURCE.json` names the commit); `AUTOFILL_API_VERSION` is 6, and the mock speaks 6 (`e2e/mock-server/mock.mjs` `MOCK_API_VERSION`; `test/contract.test.ts` checks the copy names it). An extension still speaking 5 gets 409 `conflict` on every token request; the version adds no scope, so a connection made under 5 keeps working once the extension is reloaded: no new pairing.
- **The platforms:** `ATS_KINDS` lists, after `oracle`, the new ATSs and SmartRecruiters in `PLATFORMS` order (`jobvite`, `jazzhr`, `bamboohr`, `rippling`, `breezy`, `careerplug`, `pinpoint`, `paycor`, `hibob`, `ultipro`, `dayforce`, `paylocity`, `dice`, `clearcompany`, `adp`, `smartrecruiters`). `PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT` (`core/enums.ts`) is `['site']` alone: a company's own page, which is no ATS. `test/contract.test.ts` holds `ATS_KINDS` to `PLATFORMS` without `site`, and the contract's new `ATS_NAMES` to `COPY.platforms`, name for name. Their AI classification is asked under their own platform (`ai-tasks.ts` `classifyBatches`; `site` is still withheld as unknown-field), and a saved answer keeps their platform (`row-actions.ts`; `site` without one). A company scope is still read for Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby and Workable alone (`adapters/company.ts`), though API 6 takes one such as `pinpoint:<tenant>`.
- **Addresses:** `parseAtsUrl` reads every new ATS as its adapter's `url.ts` does (platform, board, job id), and Oracle's government cloud, Workable's job-search site (`jobs.workable.com/view/<id>`, no board) and iCIMS's steps after the gate (`/candidate`, `/questions`, `/eeo`, `/form` and `/job?mode=submit_apply`). `src/adapters/url.test.ts` holds every row of its table to the strict comparison (the 45 rows that said TailorIT read none of their platform, `AHEAD_WHY`, `SR_WHY`, the government cloud's and the job-search site's, now agree), and a case fails if any address the extension reads a posting or an application from is one `parseAtsUrl` reads none of (a page after submitting aside: Greenhouse's `/confirmation`, with its reason). Which address TailorIT is asked with is the snapshot build's rule (below, "The address TailorIT is asked with").
- **Add job** is unchanged: `addablePosting` reads none of the new ATSs, nor Workable's job-search site (no posting API TailorIT may read), so the job card offers Add on none of them and POST /api/autofill/jobs is never sent for one. **Posting cards:** a new ATS's posting page (Jobvite's, Rippling's, Breezy's, Pinpoint's, UltiPro's OpportunityDetail, SmartRecruiters') is now a card frame with no form (`frames.ts` `postingAddress` reads the postings `parseAtsUrl` reads), looked up as it loads, as Workday's and iCIMS's have been since API 5.
- **Sign-in Auto: the page's path.** TailorIT's `signInHostCovered(host, path?)` covers Oracle's government cloud (`<tenant>.fa.ocs.oraclegovcloud.com`) and UltiPro (`recruiting.ultipro.com`) by their host, and ADP Workforce Now (`workforcenow.adp.com` under `/mascsr/default/mdf/recruitment/` and `/mascsr/applicant/mdf/recruitment/`) and ClearCompany (a tenant's host, never `cc-` or `www`, under `/careers/`) by their candidate paths alone: never without a path, never on another path of those hosts (an employer's employee pages), never on a path that is not plain (a query, a fragment, a dot segment, a percent-encoded dot, slash or backslash).
  - The worker (`background/sign-in.ts`) reads the path as the pathname of `from.url`, Chrome's address for the sending frame (`sender.url`, or the tab's address for a top frame on the same origin), never from the message (its schema is strict: a message carrying a path is refused). It sends the path with the host (`GET /api/autofill/credentials?host=&path=`) only where it decides the every-site sign-in, a host covered by its candidate paths alone, at one of them (`!signInHostCovered(host) && signInHostCovered(host, path)`), never from a company's own page (reach `site`), and takes `*` only where `signInHostCovered(host, path)` holds.
  - The server client (`server-client.ts` `credential(host, path?)`) sends `path` only as the contract's query takes it (from its first `/`, no `?`, `#` or space, at most 2,048 characters); any other is left out and the host asked alone, which still finds the sign-in saved for that host.
  - The content script (`content/sign-in.ts` `hostFits`) holds `*` to the page's own pathname at the typing, and `dom/sign-in-form.ts` lets a declared form its page's script sends through on an ADP or ClearCompany candidate path alone (`signInHostCovered(hostname, pathname)`).
  - The mock copies the API 6 rule (`mockSignInHostCovered`), answers `*` by it and refuses a `path` with a query or fragment (400); `test/mock-server.test.ts` holds its answers to the contract's `signInHostCovered` over hosts and paths (its old copy lacked the API 5 hosts).
- **The auto-apply agent: iCIMS and Pinpoint.** API 6 adds `icims` and `pinpoint` to `AUTOFILL_AGENT_ATS`; `agentApplicationUrl` gives iCIMS's posting page (`https://<portal>/jobs/<id>/job`, from the posting, its gate or any step after it) and Pinpoint's form (`https://<tenant>.pinpointhq.com/en/postings/<uuid>/applications/new`, from the posting, the form or the thanks page). The extension's agent needed no change: `agentAddressOf` takes any address `agentApplicationUrl` reads on an ATS host with an application key. Checked (`background/agent.test.ts`, `content/agent-api6.browser.test.ts`): every page of one application's walk is the task's own key (iCIMS's wrapper, its frame, the gate, `/candidate`, `/questions`, `/eeo`, `/job?mode=submit_apply`; Pinpoint's posting, form and thanks page); iCIMS's "Apply for this job online" (`a.iCIMS_ApplyOnlineButton`) is the adapter's entry, the gate then follows the person's Sign-in mode, a step's form gets run/start, and `.iCIMS_SuccessMessage` on `/job?mode=submit_apply` finishes the job submitted; Pinpoint's form gets run/start and `section#application-thanks` is its sign, its posting's Apply Now its entry. The ATSs API 6 did not add to the agent (Jobvite, SmartRecruiters, Breezy and the rest) are still failed `unsupported_site`.

## v3 Phase 8: company career sites (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12 Decision 13)
What follows holds over every note below where they differ. The plan's Phase 8 ("LinkedIn job pages and company career sites") as the owner narrowed it on 2026-11-06: **LinkedIn is deferred to v5** (no banner, no reader, no Apply with Autofill, no opener pairing; nothing of the extension runs on any LinkedIn host, even with every https page granted); the company career sites are built. The read-only research of 2026-11-04 (its report and evidence stay outside the repository) is the source: docs/FIXTURES.md "The company career sites".
- **The manifest** (`wxt.config.ts`): `permissions` gain `scripting`; `optional_host_permissions` is `['https://*/*']` (`shared/hosts.ts` `OPTIONAL_HOST_PERMISSIONS`), asked for only on the person's click, never `<all_urls>`, never at install; the required host permissions are unchanged (the ATS list and TailorIT), the static content scripts still the two on the ATS hosts. WXT 0.21.4 adds a content script's `matches` to the required host permissions even with `registration: 'runtime'` (`dist/core/utils/manifest.mjs`), so the sentinel is an unlisted script (`src/entrypoints/sentinel.ts`), registered at run time only.
- **Options** (`ui/options/Options.tsx` `WhereItWorks`; `COPY.options.sites`): one toggle, "Company career sites". Turning it on calls `PagePlatform.requestHosts(['https://*/*'])` inside the click, before anything is awaited (Chrome's user gesture), then `sites/set {on: true}`; a `sites/set` that fails after Chrome granted it gives the permission back at once (`PagePlatform.removeHosts`: the Phase 8 review, P8). Off sends `sites/set {on: false}`, and the worker gives the permission back (`permissions.remove`). `sites/get` answers `{on, granted}`; the toggle shows checked only with both, and says so when Chrome's permission was taken back.
- **Where it never runs** (`shared/hosts.ts` `isCompanySiteUrl`, the one rule the sentinel's recognition, the worker's check and the site adapter read): an http page; an `ATS_MATCHES` page (`onAtsMatch`: its static content script runs there, and an ATS domain's other hosts stay dormant); LinkedIn (`LINKEDIN_HOST_MATCHES`: `*.linkedin.com`, `*.linkedin.cn`, `*.lnkd.in`, `*.licdn.com`; deferred to v5); and the employee HR systems (`HR_HOST_MATCHES`: all of `*.oraclecloud.com`, `*.myworkday.com`, `*.sapsf.com`, `*.sapsf.eu`; `*.successfactors.eu` is an ATS match already). **The principle: a system where a person works for their employer (its HR, payroll, leave and expenses) never gets the sentinel, nor the content script**; a candidate page on such a domain is an ATS match the static script serves (Oracle's `/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/`, Workday's `myworkdayjobs.com`, SuccessFactors' `career<n>` hosts).
- **The registration** (`background/sites.ts`; `shared/hosts.ts` `registrationsFor`, pure): the person's flag (`storage.local` `sites:v1`) and Chrome's permission (`permissions.contains`) give `registrationsFor({sites})`: none while off; while on, `tailorit-sites`, `js: ['sentinel.js']`, `matches: ['https://*/*']`, `excludeMatches` every `ATS_MATCHES` pattern, every LinkedIn host and every HR system's, `allFrames: false`, `document_idle`, `persistAcrossSessions`, the isolated world. `reconcile()` (one at a time, a keyed queue) reads Chrome's (`getRegisteredContentScripts`), unregisters one that differs and registers what is missing; it runs at the worker's start (`sites.start()`, after the injected documents are loaded into memory), at install, on `permissions.onAdded`/`onRemoved` (`HostPermissions.onChanged`) and after `sites/set`.
- **Stopping** (the Phase 8 review, P1/B8): once a reconcile finds the career sites no longer act (turned off, Chrome's permission taken back, in Options or in Chrome's own settings, even while the worker was stopped: `storage.session` `sites:acting` remembers they acted), the worker forgets every injected document (memory and every `injected:*` key), `InjectedOf.now` and `read` answer null (the router and the resume port refuse those frames), and every tab's top frame is told `site/stop` (`tabs.ids`: `tabs.query({})`, the ids alone; `sendToFrame(tab, {v: 1, type: 'site/stop'}, {frameId: 0})`). A page the worker injected (the content runtime, from the worker alone: `fromWorker`) ends its run (`cancelRun`) and removes its UI (`teardown`); a running sentinel stops (`platform/sentinel.ts` `onStop`, our id and no tab); a page on an ATS host ignores it.
- **The sentinel** (`src/sentinel/index.ts`, `recognise.ts`; `platform/sentinel.ts`): in the top frame only, never in a private tab (`extension.inIncognitoContext`), dormant: it reads the page and its address and writes nothing, adds no listener on the page (its one listener is the extension's own message channel, for the worker's `site/stop`; its timers are `dom/time.ts`'s; its watch a MutationObserver), and sends nothing until it recognises the page. It reads at load, after each address change (read every `SENTINEL_POLL_MS`, 1 s), and whenever the page adds a form, a control or JSON-LD, for the document's life (B5): a batch of mutations is looked at by tag and attribute alone (`dom/page-signals.ts` `addsFormParts`: at most `LOOK_NODES`, 200, added nodes and `LOOK_ELEMENTS`, 1,000, elements walked in them; past either the batch asks for a reading at once rather than walk on: round 2, M1), never its text or layout. Each reading waits for the browser's next frame (`dom/time.ts` `nextFrame`: a hidden tab draws none, so it is never read until shown) and for its gap: `SENTINEL_SETTLE_MS` (750 ms) after the reading before, doubling with each reading of the same address up to `SENTINEL_MAX_GAP_MS` (12 s) (P2). It stops for good once the worker took it, after `SENTINEL_MAX_ASKS` (3) asks in the document, on `site/stop`, or once the extension is gone (`platform.alive`: no runtime id; a send that throws) (P7). Recognised (`recognisePage`; never off `isCompanySiteUrl`), it sends `page/recognised {reason}` (`shared/recognition.ts` `RECOGNITION_REASONS`, in order: `career-site` `CAREER_SITES` host and posting or application path for Amazon, Apple, Google, Meta, Walmart, TikTok and Tesla (Tesla's unverified: Akamai answered 403); `platform` Avature's portal meta tags, Phenom's `data-ph-id` or assets, TalentBrew's assets, Eightfold's branding or assets; `job-posting` JSON-LD naming a JobPosting, the first `JSON_LD_BLOCKS` (4) blocks of `JSON_LD_CHARS` (64,000) characters at most, read for recognition only; `application-form` the score below). An embedded ATS (a frame on an ATS host, Greenhouse's job_board loader or `#grnhse_app`, Ashby's embed, or the address's gh_jid, ashby_jid, gh_src, LeverAppId) is no reason (B3): its own frame gets the static content script, which serves the form; the address's ATS parameter counts only as the score's job context. Its bundle (about 15 KB) holds no React, no zod and no DOM write: check-build holds it to 17 KiB (16 KiB until the new ATSs' sixteen match patterns, which it holds to rule their pages out: 16,917 bytes then) and to none of those names; lint holds its imports to the form score, the page signals, the timers, the host list and the reasons; `test/entrypoints.test.ts` lists them, and holds the score to the page signals and the page signals to the host list.
- **The application-form score** (`dom/form-score.ts` `applicationFormOf(doc, href, options)`, the one reading of the sentinel and the site adapter; `dom/page-signals.ts`). The containers: each form, and each tree's controls outside any form (against the body, or a shadow root's host), open shadow roots included (the scanner's traversal, at most 16 roots among the first 20,000 elements: B9). A container is claimed (B1, the review's variant B) when a person can see in it either:
  - **a résumé or CV upload** (B2): a file input seen whose own words (name, id, aria-label, the elements its aria-labelledby names) say résumé, resume, CV or curriculum vitae; or a seen label of it (its labels, its aria-labelledby) saying so; or its short surroundings (up to three levels, `AROUND_CAP` 160 characters, a longer text being the page's) naming one as a noun ("Upload your resume", "Resume/CV", "Résumé", a title "Resume *"), never the verb ("uploads pause and resume"); a hidden file input counts only through such a label, a seen drop zone or button around it (by its tag, role, class, id or data-role, or its own words asking for a file), or its labelled group (Greenhouse's `role=group` labelled "Resume/CV"); a drop zone or upload button of the container whose own short text names a résumé counts when the page holds a file input (a Dropzone's input on the body; a button's text 60 characters at most);
  - **all three of a name, an email and a phone box on a page about a job** (round 2 of the review, J1): a job signal of the page (an ATS parameter, a career site's address, a platform's fingerprint, a JobPosting) or an unambiguous job word (careers, jobs, job, vacancy, vacancies, requisition, recruiting, recruitment) in a label of its host or a segment of its path (`addressNamesAJob`); never its title or a heading elsewhere on the page (a footer's "Careers", an article on hiring);
  - **two of them in a container that names a job itself** (`containerNamesAJob`): its own legend, headings and buttons, or the heading just above it, name a job: those words, a résumé or CV, or the ambiguous words only in a job's phrase (a talent community, network or pool; we're or now hiring; a candidate profile; an application for the position; open positions; an employment application). "Hire top talent", "Meet the candidates", "an employment verification" and "the positions workshop" name none. The controls outside any form are read around them (their nearest common ancestor below the body).
  "Apply" and "application" alone never count. A name box is read per part of its words (so `name=name` or a label "Name" counts; a company's, a user's, a school's, a reference's never).
  A card payment (a card number, CVV or CVC, autocomplete `cc-*`), a search form (role=search, or an action, id, name, label or class saying search) or a comment form (its id, class, name or action saying comment, as WordPress's `#commentform` and `wp-comments-post` do, or a textarea named "comment" or labelled "Comment", "Leave a comment": J1; an application's "Comments" box is none) rules its container out, a search box inside a bigger container is passed over, and a payment provider's frame anywhere on the page (Stripe, Braintree, Adyen, PayPal, Square, Checkout.com, Shopify's card fields and others, or a frame titled card or payment) rules the page out (P5). A password rules out only a sign-in (B9): the group around it (its nearest ancestor holding another control) without a résumé upload or a name and a phone; a header sign-in inside a page-wide form (ASP.NET) is passed over and the rest of the form scored; "create a password to track your application" beside a name, a phone or a résumé is no sign-in. A page that embeds an ATS (its frame or its loader) claims nothing (B3). Seen means rendered, at least `MIN_CONTROL_PX` (8) either way, on the page's canvas and at least 0.1 opaque with its ancestors, a label at least 1 px (P4); the site adapter adds the scanner's own visibility (`isUserVisible`, camouflage). Every read is bounded (P2): 400 controls, the words before any layout (a control its words do not name is never measured), each element's text read once and bounded (`textOf`: 64 text nodes, the characters it needs, never a whole subtree's `textContent`), each tree's labels listed once. **Known limit:** a form in a frame of the page's own origin is that frame's document, which the top frame's sentinel does not read (a page whose only form is there is not recognised by it). The review's corpus (`fixtures/synthetic/sites/corpus`, 32 ordinary pages, round 2's nine with job words around them among them, and 12 application pages: `src/dom/form-score.browser.test.ts`): no ordinary page is claimed or recognised; 9 of the 12 application pages are (not "Apply for Barista" at `/join-us`, which names no job; nor the same-origin frame's form; nor, since J1, a multi-step application's first step on a jobs host showing a name and an email alone, whose form names no job itself: its address takes all three). Against the capture: Amazon's posting and sign-in, Walmart's email gate, Intuit's sign-in box (its password) and Netflix's "Let your resume/CV search for jobs" (its upload and buttons name no résumé; the banner around it is no drop zone) are not claimed; the real forms of the ATS captures (Greenhouse's, Lever's, Ashby's, Workable's), standing in for one served on a company's page, are.
- **The worker's check and the injection** (`sites.ts` `recognised`): at most `RECOGNISED_LIMIT` (10) a minute per tab; only from the sentinel's channel (`senders.ts` below), frame 0, not incognito, with a document id; the address Chrome gives (the tab's, when on the document's origin) must be a company site (`isCompanySiteUrl`), and the career sites must act. Then the document is recorded (`storage.session` `injected:<tab>:<documentId>` = `{frameId, kind: 'site', at}`, and in memory) before `scripting.executeScript({target: {tabId, documentIds: [documentId]}, files: ['content-script.js']})` runs, once per document (a refusal by Chrome forgets the record). The record and the injection run one at a time with the reconcile (its keyed queue) and read again whether the career sites act (round 2, M2): a stop either comes first, and nothing is injected, or after, and forgets that document and tells its page too. A tab keeps its last `TAB_DOCUMENTS` (4) documents' records (B7: a page the back/forward cache brings back is still the site's). Nothing goes to TailorIT. The e2e build notes `page-recognised {reason, tabId, injected}` in `e2e:events`, its writes one at a time (a keyed queue), so two frames' events never overwrite each other.
- **Who may talk to the worker** (`senders.ts`, `router.ts`): `channelOf` is unchanged (synchronous: `panel/open` still reaches `sidePanel.open` with nothing awaited). A sender it refuses that is an https frame of a tab with our id and an active document (`offAtsSenderOf`) is, when the worker injected that document of that tab and frame while the career sites act (`InjectedOf.now` from memory, `read` from storage after a restart; null otherwise), a content channel with `reach: 'site'`, which sends what an ATS frame sends; else, for `page/recognised` from frame 0 alone, `reach: 'sentinel'`. `allowedOn` holds the sentinel's channel to `page/recognised` and every other to anything but it. After a restart, before memory is loaded, `panel/open` from such a top frame opens the side panel before storage is read, so the launcher's click still counts (`RouterOptions.openSidePanel`: B6). The resume port takes an injected site frame too (its messages held, `heldPort`, while a restarted worker reads the injection from storage).
- **The content script on a company page** (`entrypoints/content-script.ts`: one instance per document, a mark in the isolated world; `adapters/site`; `adapters/index.ts`): `applicationOf` reads a company site's address as the `site` application of its origin and path (`site:<hex16>`), `adaptersFor` gives it the site adapter alone: the generic adapter's standard semantics, but a page claimed by the form score only (`siteFormOf`, with the scanner's visibility), watched for a form it draws for the document's life (`late-form.ts` `watchForSiteForm`: the same tag-and-attribute filter, each look at the next frame after a gap of `SITE_LOOK_GAP_MS`, 750 ms, doubling up to 12 s: B5); no step logic (final 'unknown', nothing pressed). The runtime says no hello until the site adapter claims a form (`runtime.ts` `hello`), but a top frame a job signal recognised (a career site, a platform, a JobPosting) and that embeds no ATS says a form-less hello, so the side panel shows "No application form" (B10); a page the back/forward cache brings back says hello again, and if the worker refuses it (the career sites stopped while it was away: `site/stop` reaches active documents alone) it ends its run and removes its UI (`runtime.ts` `restored`: round 2, M3); a page that embeds an ATS form leaves it to that frame's own static script. `PLATFORMS` gains `site`, in `PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT` (never named to TailorIT: its fields' AI classification is withheld, a saved answer keeps no platform); `COPY.platforms.site` "Company site"; the frame registry names it (`frames.ts`); `keyFitsFrame` holds an https frame off the ATS hosts to `site:` keys. The page-world bridge and the forged-event listener never run there (`bridgeInstallsAt` reads no such address). Sign-in Auto gives a company page a sign-in saved for its exact host alone, never the every-site one (`sign-in.ts`, `reach: 'site'`: P6).
- **What TailorIT hears of a company page** (P3): its address only when the person opens the side panel on it (the job card's lookup) or starts Autofill (the run's lookup, the resume choice), never as it loads (`job-cards.ts` `recognisedPosting` is false for a `site` address before anything else, a `?gh_jid=` TailorIT's parser reads included). With the panel already open, a claimed page's lookup as it loads counts as the person having opened it. Every lookup, job add and applied report names a company page by its origin and path, and the `gh_jid` or `ashby_jid` that names its posting (a short id), nothing else (`adapters/site` `addressForTailorIT`, applied at the one door, `server-client.ts` `lookup`, `addJob`, `applied`).
- **The side panel** (`reports.ts` `pageNow`, `Panel.tsx` `Unsupported`): with no frame of ours on the tab, while the career sites act and the tab shows a company site (`tabs.companySite`: a flag from the tab's address, never the address), it says "No application form found here yet" with a reload hint (`PageState.reload` and `site`: B10); its "not supported" view says where Company career sites works.
- **What the worker reads of tabs:** with `https://*/*` granted, Chrome gives the worker every https tab's address in `tabs.get`, `tabs.query` and `tabs.onUpdated`. The worker reads one only where it needs it, as a flag: `onAtsHost`/`atsPageOf` (the reload hint on an ATS host), `companySite` (the reload hint on a company page), `onLoaded` (the auto-apply agent's own tab), and `hostOf` (the host alone, for "Ask TailorIT to support this site" on the person's click); `tabs.ids` reads ids alone. None of these is stored or sent, but `hostOf`'s host on that click.
- **Tests:** `src/shared/hosts.test.ts` (the registration and its exclusions as Chrome reads them, `isCompanySiteUrl` the same, LinkedIn's and the HR systems' hosts, `onAtsMatch` against Chrome's match patterns, the career sites), `src/background/sites-worker.test.ts` (the registration reconciled at start, on the permission's changes and on the toggle, a stale one replaced; the injection, its re-check, its limits, a refused one forgotten, a restart; the router's new channels; stopping on the toggle, on a revoke and after a restart; a tab's documents; the side panel's click after a restart; what TailorIT hears; a company page's sign-in; the reload hint), `src/dom/form-score.browser.test.ts` (the review's corpus, the capture, the rule, the résumé upload both ways, what rules a form out, what a person can see, bounded reads), `src/sentinel/recognise.browser.test.ts` and `sentinel.browser.test.ts` (the signals, no embed alone, the sentinel dormant, once, a form drawn after 30 s, its gaps, no reading for mutations that add no form, the next frame, a careers site's own routes, the stops, LinkedIn and the HR systems never, no DOM write), `src/content/site-runtime.browser.test.ts` (claims, the form-less hello, a late form, `site/stop`), `src/ui/options/options.browser.test.tsx` (the toggle, the permission given back), `src/ui/panel/look.browser.test.tsx` (the reload hint), `src/adapters/index.test.ts` and `src/background/job-cards.test.ts` (the address TailorIT hears), `test/check-build.test.ts`, `test/entrypoints.test.ts`, and `e2e/specs/43-company-sites.spec.ts` (a profile seeded with the permission, `e2e/helpers/sites.ts`, since Chrome's permission dialog answers no automation: the toggle and the registration, turning it off ending an injected page, a Greenhouse embed beside a talent-community form with nothing injected and only the Greenhouse form filled, a company form claimed and dry-run as the site, an ordinary site silent with the sentinel listening for nothing, LinkedIn with nothing of the extension at all).

## v3 Phases 6 and 7b: Workday, iCIMS, Taleo, SuccessFactors and Oracle, their public pages (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12, Decision 12)
Five adapters, each written from the read-only public capture of 2026-11-04/05 (docs/FIXTURES.md "The Workday, iCIMS, Taleo, SuccessFactors and Oracle capture": 14 tenants' postings, entries and account pages, nothing typed, no account made, every non-GET request aborted), for what is public: the posting, the entry, and the sign-in, create-account, password-help and email-gate pages. The application's steps behind the account needed a signed-in session: the owner's snapshots of 2026-11-06 gave Workday's, iCIMS's and Oracle's, whose hooks are "The snapshot build of 2026-11-06" below, which holds over this section where they differ; Taleo's and SuccessFactors' still get the standard rules (the generic adapter's), and SuccessFactors' form after the account (`/portalcareer`, an address that names no job) is not read at all. Every hook names its fixture; DOM tests hold each to its case's hand-written expectations (`fixtures/<platform>/*.expect.json`, their `hooks` entry: docs/FIXTURES.md).
- **Hosts** (`src/shared/hosts.ts`): `ATS_MATCHES` gains `https://*.icims.com/*`, `https://*.taleo.net/*`, `https://*.successfactors.com/*`, `https://*.successfactors.eu/*` and a path-scoped `https://*.oraclecloud.com/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/*` (a Chrome pattern wildcards only a host's first labels; check-build follows the list, and Chrome reads Oracle's host permission as all of oraclecloud.com: docs/PRIVACY.md). `HOST_PLATFORMS` names the candidate hosts alone (`careers-<co>.icims.com` and `<co>.icims.com` but www, api, login and internal-*; `<co>.taleo.net` but www and Taleo Business Edition; `career<n>.successfactors.*`; `<tenant>.fa[.<region>].oraclecloud.com`): on every other host of those domains the script is dormant (no platform, no application). Workday's two were already there.
- **Ahead of the contract:** `PLATFORMS` gains `icims`, `taleo`, `successfactors` and `oracle`, all four in `PLATFORMS_AHEAD_OF_CONTRACT` (`core/enums.ts`) until TailorIT's `ATS_KINDS` lists them: their AI classification is not asked (`ai-tasks.ts` `classifyBatches` withholds it as unknown-field), a saved answer keeps no platform (`row-actions.ts`) and no company scope (`company.ts` reads none of their addresses); `test/contract.test.ts` holds `ATS_KINDS` to `PLATFORMS` without them and fails once TailorIT lists one. Their addresses go to TailorIT's lookup as any recognised posting's (`job-cards.ts` `recognisedPosting`, Workday's exclusion dropped too): TailorIT's `parseAtsUrl` reads none of the five, so the card says the posting is not in TailorIT and offers no Add (`addablePosting`), and TailorIT's agent never queues one (`agentApplicationUrl`). `frames.ts` `postingAddress` keeps a posting page with no form the card frame only where `parseAtsUrl` reads it.
- **Addresses** (each `url.ts`, pure, its own table and the shared one in `src/adapters/url.test.ts`, every row saying TailorIT reads it not yet):
  - Workday: `<tenant>.wd<n>.myworkdayjobs.com/[<locale>/]<site>/job/<location>/<slug>_<reqId>` (also `<tenant>.myworkdayjobs.com`, as `HOST_PLATFORMS` names it; and `wd<n>.myworkdaysite.com/[<locale>/]recruiting/<tenant>/<site>/job/...`; the locale in any case, two or three letters and a region or script of two to four, and only where a site follows it); the posting and `/apply` (its choice) are 'posting', `/apply/applyManually` the application and so are `/apply/autofillWithResume` and `/apply/useMyLastApplication` (the person's own choice, never pressed by the extension, under the same key), `/login`, `/userHome` and `/details/...` none. The key drops the free-text location (the apply links re-encode it) and folds the site (case-insensitive) and the slug's percent-encoding; canonical `https://<host>/<site>/job/x/<slug>_<reqId>/apply/applyManually` (verified to load the account landing).
  - iCIMS: `/jobs/<id>[/<slug>]/job` the posting (the wrapper and its `?in_iframe=1` frame alike), `/login` the gate (never "Log back in", `login?loginOnly=1`); only those words were read until the owner's snapshots of 2026-11-06 named the pages after the gate (the v3 Phase 6/7b review, C11): since then `candidate`, `questions`, `eeo` and `form` are the application and `job?mode=submit_apply` its confirmation, and an IForm's own frame on `<co>.icims.com/forms` is keyed by the tab's chain ("The snapshot build of 2026-11-06"); `/referral` stays none; the key is the host and the id (slug and query dropped).
  - Taleo: `/careersection/<cs>/jobdetail.ftl?job=<id>` the posting, `jobapply.ftl?job=<id>` the application, the account pages under `/careersection/iam/accessmanagement/*.jsf` (and the Privacy Agreement's) by the job their `redirectionURI` or `TARGET` names (encoded, or written plain as the forgot links do, its `job=` then the page's own where `lang=` comes first), `flow.jsf` and `application.jss` only where they name the job; the key is the host, the career section as written (no capture shows the server folding its case) and the job id.
  - SuccessFactors: `/career`, its query deciding: `career_ns=job_listing` without a login view is the posting, any other with `career_job_req_id` and a company the application (the sign-in's `loginFlowRequired=true`, `login_ns=register|forgot_pwd`), the per-load `_s.crb`, `requestParams` and `jobSecKey` dropped; a page after a post (`/career?_s.crb=...`) names no job, so none.
  - Oracle: `/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/<lang>/sites/<site>/job/<id>` (and `/requisitions/preview/<id>`) the posting, anything under `.../job/<id>/apply` the application (the email gate, `isOracleEmailGate`, and the steps) and `.../job/<id>/easy-apply` too, `/my-profile/sign-in` none; the key drops the language and folds the site to upper case. A tenant's pages outside `/hcmUI/CandidateExperience/` are no platform's (`platformOfUrl`: an employee's Fusion pages get no reload hint).
- **The adapters** (`src/adapters/<platform>/index.ts`), what each knows:
  - **Workday:** the root is `main > [data-automation-id=applyFlowPage]`, the flow page holding the account view (step 1 of its progress bar) and, by that bar, every later step in the same document (late: `quirks.formLateMs` 30 s); `postingText` `[data-automation-id=jobPostingDescription]`; `entryControl` the posting's `a[data-automation-id=adventureButton]` (`.../apply`, waited for 15 s: `quirks.entryWaitMs`), then, once the choice shows in place (`[role=dialog][data-automation-id=wd-popup-frame]`), its `a[data-automation-id=applyManually]` (`quirks.entryPresses` 2, both `entryNeverPressed`); `authState` 'entry-choice' while the choice shows, then in `signInContent` 'forgot-password' (its `resetPasswordButton`), 'create-account' (`createAccountSubmitButton`), 'sign-in' (`signInSubmitButton`, or the landing's "Sign in with email"); `signInForm` the `form[data-automation-id=signInFormo]` (no method, no action: `scriptSubmits`), its `email`, `password` and Create Account's `verifyPassword`, its press control the visible `div[data-automation-id=click_filter][role=button]` beside the aria-hidden submit button (`signInSubmitButton` or `createAccountSubmitButton`: its own identity), the form itself as `SignInForm.form`, and Create Account's `createAccountCheckbox` as its terms (required nowhere, drawn at opacity 0 behind its `label[for]`); `signInPrelude` the landing's `button[data-automation-id=SignInWithEmailButton]`; `neverPress` the header's Sign In, menus and navigation, "View all jobs", similar jobs, Read more, social and privacy links, the choice's Autofill with Resume, Use My Last Application, Apply with LinkedIn and Close, Apple's and Google's sign-ins, Back to Job Posting, the view switches and Reset Password with its click_filter. The beecatcher honeypot (1 by 0 px) is never a field and never typed into. The application's steps after the account (My Information to Review): "The snapshot build of 2026-11-06" below.
  - **iCIMS:** the top page is the company's wrapper, never a root; the posting and the gate are in `iframe#icims_content_iframe` (same host, all frames). The root was the gate, `form#enterEmailForm`, alone until the snapshot build added the steps after it (`authState` 'email-gate'; `signInForm` its `input#email`, the GDPR box `#accept_gdpr` where a portal asks, and Next by its own id, `input#enterEmailSubmitButton`, a POST to the portal itself, which GDMS's page enables once the box is ticked (`enablesWhenFilled`): since the snapshot build, in the top frame or the frame that claims the wrapper's application, `iframe#icims_content_iframe`); `postingText` `.iCIMS_JobContent`; `entryControl` the visible `a.iCIMS_ApplyOnlineButton` whose address is this posting's with `mode=apply` (the agent's entry runs in the frame that claims the application: the wrapper's frame since the snapshot build); `neverPress` the share buttons and widgets, the referral, "Log back in" (login.icims.com, iCIMS's Auth0 sign-in, which names no job and which the every-site sign-in does not cover), the FAQ and "Powered by" links, and Apply. The invisible hCaptcha bound to Next does not pause; one it raises comes before the gate (`detectPause` reads a challenge first: the review's S1), so it is the person's. The wrappers converted around the gate's frame are tested never to be a root or a form, by this adapter or the generic one.
  - **Taleo:** the root is the account pages' `form#dialogTemplate-dialogForm` while it holds the login (its password box) or the password help (`#dialogTemplate-dialogForm-email`/`-username`, no password); the Privacy Agreement (`...StatementBeforeAuthentificationContent-ContinueButton`) is no root: `authState` 'entry-choice', and its I Accept is the agent's `entryControl` there (a required consent to apply, pressed in either Consents mode; I Decline never: a decision of the coordinator's, docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md); `authState` 'sign-in', 'forgot-password' for the others; `signInForm` the login's "User Name" box (`#dialogTemplate-dialogForm-login-name1`, autocomplete off: Taleo's user name, often the email; the saved email is typed there), its password and its `input[type=button]` Login, which the standard rule cannot read (no autocomplete, no submit control); `requiredFor` the red icon (`img.iam-entity-label-mandatory`) before a label; `postingText` `.editablesection`; `entryControl` the visible `input[type=button][id$="APPLY_ON_REQ.row1"]` (Apply Online, a post-back); `neverPress` New User, Apply as Guest, I Accept (the entry door's alone) and I Decline, the forgot links and the help's OK and Cancel, LinkedIn and Google sign-ins, the header's actions and Apply Online.
  - **SuccessFactors:** the root is `form#careerform` while it shows the sign-in (`#username` beside `#password`), the account's creation (`#fbclc_pwd`) or the password help (`input[name=cEmail]`); `authState` 'sign-in', 'create-account', 'forgot-password'; `signInForm` the sign-in (the standard rule reads the same: `autocomplete=username`/`current-password`; declared, its button is the typeless one whose words read "Sign In") and the account's creation (its `#fbclc_userName`, `#fbclc_emailConf` as `confirmEmail`, `#fbclc_pwd`, `#fbclc_pwdConf`, and `button#fbclc_createAccountButton`, `personPresses`: the form also asks for a name, a phone, a country and terms in a dialog, the person's, so the credential is typed and the press is theirs); `postingText` `.joqReqDescription`; `entryControl` `button#applyButton_top`, which sits in the page's form and would submit it, so the entry door never presses it (the agent will open the Sign In address instead, once TailorIT hands it one); `neverPress` Apply, the cookie buttons, Show, the help's buttons, the terms dialog's opener and the employees' links.
  - **Oracle:** the root is the flow's content, `main[aria-labelledby=apply-flow-main-heading] section.apply-flow__content`, holding the gate and, by the flow, the steps after it (late: 30 s); `authState` 'email-gate' at the gate's address (`isOracleEmailGate`, `.../apply/email`) with its `input[name=primary-email]` in the flow, or 'email-code' by the code page's own six `input.pin-code-input__input` boxes (the owner's snapshot of 2026-11-06; Jobright's reading, four or more one-character boxes, was dropped); `signInForm` (wall 'email-gate') that gate where its form also holds what only the gate has (its CANCEL, `[data-qa=emailVerificationCancelButton]`, and its honeypot `input[name=honey-pot]`, aria-hidden and out of the tab order, never typed into): its email (`colourUnknown`: drawn over the flow's image), its required `#legal-disclaimer-checkbox` and the submit button whose title, aria-label or words read "Next" (`enablesWhenFilled`), in a form with no method and no action (`scriptSubmits`); the terms box's label reads over that image on the captured tenants, which Sign-in Auto refused ('hidden') until core pass Y bounded a translucent panel over an image (the snapshot build): the gate is now taken as captured; `postingText` the first `.job-details__description-content`; `entryControl` the first visible `button.apply-now-button` (a route change in place, after which the gate is drawn there and the late watch finds it); `neverPress` APPLY NOW, CANCEL, the dialogs' buttons, the map's controls, the chat and the copyright links. There is no password anywhere: identity is the emailed code, the person's. JPMC's region-less host is covered by the every-site sign-in since TailorIT's API 5, and the government cloud's tenants since API 6.
- **Sign-in Auto** (`dom/sign-in-form.ts`, `content/sign-in.ts`, `dom/fillers/not-submit.ts`; Decision 12, as the v3 Phase 6/7b review held it): a declared form (`SignInForm.declared`, set by `signInFormOf`) is the adapter's own form element (`SignInForm.form`, every box and the button in it; no form owner, refused), names its button by an identity of its own, and may be one its page's script sends (`scriptSubmits`: no method, no action, on https on a covered host); its press control may be any element with role=button inside the form; a disabled button refuses it, as it does a standard form, unless the adapter says its page enables it once filled (`enablesWhenFilled`), when it is waited for `SIGN_IN_ENABLE_MS` (3 s) after typing; its terms are at most one unrequired box the adapter declares (same form, shown, or reached through a visible label whose words read clear, under no aria-hidden); its boxes keep every honeypot rule but those the adapter names in `colourUnknown` (never a password box: Oracle's gate email, in front of the flow's background image), which skip the colour test alone; `confirmEmail` is typed with the email; `personPresses` types the credential and leaves the press to the person (`continue`). A CAPTCHA shown and not solved comes before every account page (`detectPause`), so nothing is asked or typed while one shows; the page is looked at for one right before the typing and the press, and one the press raises is handed over (`continue`). Each password write is preceded by a fresh reading of its box (`passwordBoxIn`: connected, a password box, the same form). From the typing to the end of the watch `guardSubmission` (on the window, in the capture phase) cancels every native submission of a script-sent form, and of any other one that would not go as a POST to the page's origin (its submitter's formaction and formmethod read); on every outcome but `continue` and `left` the password typed is written empty again. Round 3 of the review: a submission the guard allowed keeps its password in its own entry list (the `formdata` event names no submitter, so the guard marks the allowed submission and clears the mark once that list is read or at the next microtask, X6); a declared terms box is ticked only for a visible label whose words read clear, shown or not (X7); Sign-in Auto also waits out any visible frame of another origin whose address, title, name or aria-label reads like a challenge (`pause-detect.ts` `challengeLikeFramesShown`: captcha, challenge, verify, human, arkose, turnstile, or the word bot), which a fill does not pause for (X9).
- **An opaque layer hides what is under it, failing closed** (round 2 of the review, E2 and K2: a layer counts only where it is visible, and nothing from it up, nor between it and the content, is transformed (transform, rotate, scale, translate, perspective, zoom) or draws an inset box-shadow, an outline or a border image; content under a negative z-index that applies, positioned or a flex or grid item, is unknown; the plain compositing paints no background for a hidden element, counts an inset shadow as one more colour behind the content and a border image as unknown) (`dom/camouflage.ts` `backdropOf`, `opaqueLayerUnder`): where an image below makes the backdrop unknown, the nearest opaque background colour with no image, at full opacity, with no rounded corner and under no effect, whose painted area (its background-clip's box) contains the content judged (a text's own box, a Range over its own text nodes; a control's box), is all that shows behind that content. A layer the content overflows or lies outside (one of no height, content positioned outside it) or content under a negative z-index stays unknown (the review's S4: an AI trap, a positioned escape, a honeypot). Taleo's pages put a white body over an html background image, which no colour can be worked out of: every word there read as hidden (failing closed), so its posting could not be read for the AI policy and its labels were none (the live check of 2026-11-05). A translucent layer, one faded by opacity, one with an image of its own or under a blend, filter or mask stays unknown (`camouflage.browser.test.ts`). A wall whose form shows after a choice gets its declared prelude pressed first (`clickAdapterSignInPrelude`, the fourth door, sign-in.ts alone: `eslint.config.mjs`, `test/allow-submit.test.ts`), the form waited for `SIGN_IN_PRELUDE_MS` (10 s). The wall `email-gate` (`SIGN_IN_WALLS`, the machine's `AuthWall`) types the email alone: the worker answers it with no password (`background/sign-in.ts`). `clickAdapterSignIn` refuses a never-pressed control anywhere on the page. A password reset ('forgot-password', `PAUSE_KINDS`, `PAUSE_REASONS`) is a handoff, its copy "This page resets your password, which only you can do"; the agent reads it as sign_in.
- **Where each layer paints** (round 3 of the review, X2 to X5; `dom/camouflage.ts` `zUnder`, `heldUnder`, `clipOf`, `turned`, `cornersOver`, `shadowReaches`, `dedupe`): a negative z-index applies by the layout parent (past `display: contents`), an old flexbox (`-webkit-box`, `-webkit-inline-box`) counting as a flexbox (X2). In the plain compositing (`heldUnder`), `display: contents` paints nothing, and each layer is composited from the canvas in only as far as it paints under what of the content shows at it (what every box below that clips its overflow lets show: a scrolling box brings its content into view, so a long list's rows read; a hidden overflow that cuts all of it, a collapsed "Read more", a 1 px screen-reader box, a skip link above the page, leaves it unknown): a layer whose painted box (by background-clip, outside its rounded corners' arcs) covers all of it replaces the colours below, one that covers none of it adds nothing (a layer of no height the text overflows, a background clipped to the content box with the text in the padding, text positioned past its card: each read against what it is over), one that covers a part adds its colours as candidates (a narrow layer the text runs out of); a turned, skewed or 3D layer that changes the backdrop is unknown (X3). Failing closed on every layer that does not hold the content (the decision's first reading) read trap 02's paragraph a person scrolls to, spliced into Lever's form, as unknown: `spliced.browser.test.ts` caught it. An outline drawn inside a box over the content (a negative offset) and a text decoration over 3 px are unknown (X3). An inset shadow adds its colour only where its band reaches the content (a ring, an underline bar or a vignette inset by padding adds none), and colours within 2 of each other per channel are one before the cap of 16 (X5). A background gradient replaces the colours under it only where its computed size, position and repeat make it paint the whole box (repeated, or auto, cover, contain or 100% at a percentage or zero offset); one of no size adds nothing, and any other (a band, one pushed aside) adds its colours beside those under it (round 4, Y2, `gradientCover`); a control's own gradient draws it only where it paints at all in a colour that shows. The opaque layer takes axis-aligned transforms (a matrix with b = c = 0 and a, d > 0, a depth translation: translate, translateZ, a positive scale), zoom, outlines drawn outside the box (offset 0 or more) and rounded layers whose corners' arcs the content stays inside (`cornersOver`: each corner's radii scaled down together where neighbours overlap, as CSS draws them, so a pill's 25 px on a box 22 px high is 11 px); it fails closed on rotate, skew, 3D and perspective alone (X4). Measured over the 135 fixtures (9,152 elements) against ddd532c: 228 verdicts change for the better, every one Taleo's (its pages' text now clear), and 37 for the worse, every one hidden from a person already (`isUserVisible` false, so no reader takes it today) and cut away entirely by a box that hides its overflow: Workday's collapsed "Read more" sections and a skip link above the page, iCIMS's skip links and 1 px screen-reader text, SuccessFactors' screen-reader hints, trap 02's offscreen text and a trap 04 honeypot.
- **The agent's entry** (`content/press.ts` `pressEntry`): a CAPTCHA blocks it before anything; an 'entry-choice' pause blocks it only where the adapter offers no entry control, and a press made on the choice is the entry's last; where `quirks.entryPresses` allows (at most `ENTRY_MAX_PRESSES`, 2), a press made before the choice shows is followed within `ENTRY_FOLLOW_MS` (5 s) by the control the adapter offers while it says the choice shows, never one pressed before; the answer is 'pressed' once the last press is made, a form shows or the document goes, 'blocked' by another page shown instead (a sign-in, which Sign-in Auto may then take), and 'blocked' 'other' when nothing comes. The runtime waits for the entry `entryWaitOf` the address's adapters: 8 s, or their `quirks.entryWaitMs` (Workday's and Oracle's 15 s), at most 20 s. The worker still sends `agent/enter` once per document (`agent.ts`). The job has begun once an entry answered 'pressed' (`pressedEntryAt`) or its form showed (round 2, K4); a page of an ATS host that names no posting is then `needs-you` `sign_in`, and before that a closed posting, at once: from a hello with no key, from the tab's load (`agent.loaded`, `WorkerPlatform.tabs.onLoaded` and `atsPageOf`: such a page says no hello, K3), and at the form's wait for a tab still on an ATS host (`onAtsHost`, E8); after the job's Submit, its confirmation is awaited. After Sign-in Auto signs in at the entry, the page is read again: a challenge, a code or another account page blocks the entry by its kind (E7). Round 3 (X8): `agent.loaded` looks only at the agent's own tab, reading the address the load reports (`tabs.onLoaded` hands a reading of the change's or the tab's address, read only when asked) and again after `AGENT_LOAD_SETTLE_MS` (1.5 s), so a board whose script goes on to its posting is no closed one; the page's platform comes with it (`AtsPage`), and once the job has begun a page of another platform, or one not known, is `needs-you` `other`, never `sign_in`. The content script tells the worker of the entry's press as it is made (`agent/entered`, fire-and-forget, before the entry waits on what the press brings: `pressEntry`'s `onPress`), taken from the agent's tab's top frame for a document it asked to enter, so a press that takes the document away still counts the job begun.
- **Fixtures and the converter:** `scripts/mhtml-to-fixture.mjs` takes the four new platforms and their candidate hosts, and normalises each one's per-load values (`normaliseTokens`: Workday's `input-<n>` ids and beecatcher UUID, iCIMS's `hashed=` and Auth0 `state=`, SuccessFactors' `_s.crb=`, `requestParams=` and `jobSecKey`, Oracle's `primary-email-<n>` and `honey-pot-<n>`, the hCaptcha site keys, in `data-sitekey` and in its frames' addresses, and each widget's id) in the page, its header's source and its route map. 22 cases converted (docs/FIXTURES.md); iCIMS's, Taleo's, SuccessFactors' and Oracle's keep only the style rules their own elements match (`pruneCssInPage`: the new fixtures went from 25.4 MB to 7.8 MB, every expectation unchanged); the smoke routes gain an application page on each new host (`fixtures/smoke/workday-form.html` and `oracle-form.html`, for the two late adapters); `fixtures/synthetic/workday/account.html` reproduces Workday's account views for e2e.
