The behaviour review's survivors: `src/adapters/configured/survivors.browser.test.ts` and a `survivors.browser.test.ts` per platform (Workday, SmartRecruiters, BambooHR, Dice, ClearCompany, Paylocity, Paycor, ADP, UltiPro), `src/dom/labels-repeated.browser.test.ts`, `src/core/resolver/yesNoPair.test.ts`, `src/dom/entrance-survivors.browser.test.ts` and `src/content/verify-survivors.browser.test.ts`, each rule the review's mutations showed untested pinned by a test that fails with it undone. The résumé mode: `src/content/resume-on-page.browser.test.ts` (both choices on Workday's and Rippling's snapshots of a page already showing a résumé; no Remove or Delete ever clicked), `src/content/held-resume-write.browser.test.ts`, `src/core/plan/frame.test.ts`, the settings tests (version 5 and its migration) and the options and look tests. e2e: `42-workday` ("the Workday application steps"), `44-smartrecruiters`, `45-adp`, `46-jobvite`, `49-jazzhr`, and `58-job-card`'s adds expect the posting's address. Each new rule was proven by a test that fails with the rule undone.

## v4: the owner's request of 2026-11-07 (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §13)
What follows holds over every note above and below where they differ. Three builds on shared shapes (`812db13`): the side panel (`990617d`), the run's "decide, don't flag" (`7a27ba5`) and the typeable dropdowns (`8098a1e`), merged with the coordinator's fixes (`97c7f8c`: D5 narrowed, the disclosure's version 8; `aeeb7e4`). Then the v4 review's fixes, folded into the subsections below: `PageState.choice` (`40e5e07`), the side panel (`f05259d`, merged `70e922f`), the run, worker and core (`f0a923a`, merged `181c859`) and the launcher's review tooltip (`7623a90`).

### The side panel's views (`src/ui/panel/Panel.tsx`)
The side panel (and the report page, the same component) shows one view at a time under its head:
- **The set-up view** (`Setup.tsx`): a bolt, "Autofill with TailorIT", one line, then the steps as cards, each a teal tick pill when done or a dark GO pill, each with a "?":
  1. "Set up your TailorIT account": shown once the panel has seen the extension not connected (not connected, expired, revoked; the reason in its "?"), and kept, ticked, until Start applying. GO unfolds the first-run disclosure (the options page's, word for word) with Connect, which sends `auth/startPairing` (TailorIT's connect page opens; a person with no account signs up there). While the pairing waits the pill reads "Waiting…" with the countdown and a link to the options page's typed code (`options.html#settings-connection`).
  2. "Complete your profile details": GO opens the profile editor; its "?" lists what is missing. It waits for step 1.

  Then Start applying, grey until both are done, or (the v4 review) until step 1 is, where the profile could not be read: step 2 then stays a GO whose "?" says so (`COPY.setup.profileUnreadable`). A panel that opens connected and complete goes straight to the main view; a connection lost later shows the set-up view again. A version mismatch, refused storage and the lost link stay problem strips over every view. No gear here.
- **Profile completeness** (`ui/panel/profile-check.ts` `profileGaps`): `panel/profile {sensitive: false}` (the editor's read without the sensitive part: a new optional field of the message, `messaging/schemas.ts`, honoured by `background/profile-editor.ts`), while connected, once per connection and again after the editor saved (`ProfileEditor` `onSaved`). Complete is a first name, a last name, an email (the profile's, or the account's sign-in address an empty one stands for), a phone number, and a city or a country. Only the list of what is missing is kept; a refused or failed read counts as unreadable: a panel that opens connected shows the main view, and the set-up view enables Start applying (above): the fill reads the profile itself.
- **The main view:** the head (brand left, gear right); then, only when relevant, the disclosure shown again, the agent's status line, the launcher-hidden strip, the unsupported page (its explanations in a "?"), "Application submitted"; the job card (`JobCard.tsx`: tile, company, industry (`job.industry`, from TailorIT's lookup since its v1.2 `187696e`; absent from an older TailorIT), the match pill with its explanation in the black bubble, the title with the Applied badge, "2 days ago · Austin, TX", "Not this job?" for Autofill for another job); "+ Add this job in one click" (a mint pill, `panel/addJob`) where TailorIT lacks the job and can add it; the big teal main button; "Ask custom question" (an outline button that unfolds the box); "Résumé: <label> · Preview · Change" until a run lists the résumé's own row; the feedback line; then the review (`Report notices={false}`). Gone: the gear's menu, the Dry run, Continue and Cancel buttons, the card's chips and "Already in your tracker", Save to tracker, the "What the fill uses" box, the card's cover-letter row and Write it now, and the foot ("Connected as …", Open TailorIT Jobs): the account shows in Settings.
- **The main button** (`Panel.tsx` `MainButton`; its meaning in `data-main-action`): `PageState.wall` set: "Sign up & Autofill" (`sign-up`), `panel/fill {dryRun: false, signIn: true}`, explained in a "?"; else `PageState.choice` set (the v4 review, M4; "The account wall" below): "Autofill" (`fill`), `panel/fill {dryRun: false}`, which the worker sends to the wall frame, whose run presses the declared entry, even while a run is paused; else a run paused (`handoff` or `auth-needed` alone): "Autofill" (`continue`) sends `panel/continue`; else `panel/fill {dryRun: false}` (`fill`), which also goes on with a run resting on a new step under Autofill after page turn: Manually (it reports `review`, no row and no notice; "The run goes on" below). On a posting page with no form it shows only at an entry choice or a wall, where the "No application form on this page" strip (`PageStrip`) is not shown; never on the platform's page after submitting (`confirmation`). While a run works it spins; Cancel is the progress line's. A dry run is still `panel/fill {dryRun: true}` (the e2e helper sends it), never a button.
- **The main button's hold** (the v4 review, security L4; `ui/panel/seen.ts` `useShownFor`): its meaning follows state the page controls (`PageState.wall` is set `WALL_LOOK_MS`, 300 ms, after a page shows a password box), so whenever the meaning changes (what it sends, the tab it sends to, or the button newly drawn: the panel opening, a tab switch, a return from Settings) it is disabled until the new label has been shown `CONTROL_SEEN_MS` (600 ms), then a click sends the message of the meaning it shows; a change back to an earlier meaning starts the time again. The page UI's S-1 rule and value (`content/ui/visible.ts`); `seen.ts` holds its own copy, since eslint keeps `src/ui` from importing the content script. Time is checked on animation frames (`ui/page/frames.ts` `afterFrames`), never a timer.
- **The Settings view** (`SettingsView.tsx`), the gear's: a round ✕ and "Settings"; the modes as radio-card groups (`ui/page/modes.tsx`, the options page's too, in one order: Autofill after page turn, Default view, Next page, Submit, Sign-in, Consent boxes, AI answers, Résumé already on the page, Record applications I submit myself, the skip rules), each title with its "?" (Sign-in's Auto disabled, the reason in its "?"); then "Autofill information" (the editor; its Back returns here), "Connected as …", "All settings" (the options page), and a full-width Save pinned at the bottom: grey until something changed, `settings/save {settings, base}` with the settings this view last read as `base`. ✕ drops an unsaved change; settings changed elsewhere while nothing is changed here show at once.

### The review (`src/ui/report/Report.tsx`, `model.ts`)
- **Rows** (D1, D6, D8): a teal disc with a white tick when filled, an empty grey ring when not, a spinner while written; the label shows the field on the page. No status chip, reason, provenance, matcher stage or confidence, no "AI", "AI guess" or "AI draft" mark, no alert, "Needs you", Save my answer, "N fields are left for you", AI count, "Nothing needs you" or hidden-field note. A row `shared/report.ts` `leftOut` names (a guard's refusal, and the reasons `honeypot`, `not-user-visible`, `no-visible-label`, `tripwire-in-label`) is neither listed (`shownReports`, every such row since the v4 review) nor counted, one rule for both, so the lists and the progress agree: "7/7 required fields filled | 100%" over the rows shown. A frame's line keeps its run status and step and the dry-run line, not a pause's instruction (its toast says it).
- **The in-page panel** (`content/ui/panel.tsx`) mounts the same Report with its props unchanged (`describeField`, `onConfirmDraft`), so its rows keep a drawer (Show on page, the closest options, and Confirm draft, which no row needs since D6), and its notices on top (`notices` true by default). The side panel passes `notices={false}` and neither drawer callback.
- **`ReportActions`** gains `previewOf(row)` (which file a résumé or cover-letter row previews, or null), `renderPreview(kind, label, close)` (the container's preview, drawn under the row) and `rowTool(row)` (the side panel's résumé Change after a run). Résumé and cover-letter rows (`file.resume`, `file.coverLetter`, `freeText.coverLetter`) show Preview where `previewOf` says, Show (`panel/focus`) always, and the tool. Rows carry `data-category` and `data-file` for tests.

### The cover letter's preview (`preview/request {kind: 'coverLetter'}`)
`preview/request {tabId, kind: 'coverLetter'}` on the side panel's `preview` port: `resume-transfer.ts` `acceptPreview(port, resumeFor, ready, letterFor)` resolves the letter with `index.ts` `previewLetterOf`: the ready letter (`RunLetter.readyId`) of the tab's newest fill run (not a dry run) that holds one, else the person's own letter for the tab's job (`checkLetter`, `generate` false: nothing is written; the résumé the card attaches, else the profile's). It is fetched and checked as the resume port's letter (`fetchLetter`: a PDF, its SHA-256 TailorIT's) and sent in the same acknowledged chunks; none is `resume-unavailable`. The id stays in the worker. The panel (`preview.ts` `receivePreview(…, kind)`, `ResumePreview` `kind`) shows it as it shows the résumé. Whether there is one to preview is still the card's `panel/letter {write: false}` (`ui/panel/letter.ts`); `write: true` (Write it now) has no button now.

### Tooltips (`src/ui/page/HelpTip.tsx`)
One component: a 22 px "?" ring button (`aria-label` "About <topic>", `aria-describedby` its bubble). Pointer hover (over the button or the bubble: a transparent bridge spans the gap) or keyboard focus shows a black bubble (white text, 8 px radius, at most 260 px wide), above the "?" with its arrow at it, below where there is no room, moved sideways to stay in the window; its fixed coordinates are set through the CSSOM and read again on scroll and resize while shown. Escape hides it until the pointer and the focus have both left. Every explanatory paragraph of the side panel's views, the profile editor, the options page, the disclosure-changed block and the unsupported page became one; feedback and outcome lines, errors, state lines (what a setting is now), the first-run disclosure, the plan's sentence on what never goes to the AI and the counters' list stay text. The match pill's own tip is the same black bubble.

### Decide, don't flag (D1 to D12)
- **Rows** (`shared/report.ts`): `needsPerson` holds only for a hard-rule stop (`HARD_STOP_REASONS` = `core/types/fill.ts` `PERSON_ONLY_REASONS`: `bot-check`); `notFilled` is a field the run did not fill (`rowProgressOf` 'missing', "Not filled"); `leftOut` is what D8 drops (`rowProgressOf` 'none'); `isReady` counts a flag-band fill, a "check it" fill and `prefill-kept` as filled. `leftForPerson` stays the hard-rule stop not answered yet. `unfilledByRun` (the v4 review, L-M7): a **required** field the run skipped counts as not filled whatever its status (a résumé under "Apply without resume", `resume-not-attached`; a field the page disabled, `disabled-or-readonly`, or removed, `field-disappeared`) in the progress, the lists, `lastFill.flagged`, auto-Next and Submit Auto's required-empty rule (`submit-held-required`), until the person's input on it (`orchestrator.ts` `open`) makes it filled by them; an optional skipped field stays out of the counts. A row's `status` and `needsUserAction` stay the core's (the planner keeps `status: 'flagged'` on a written flag-band row; trap specs 20, 21 and 29 read them); no count, badge, hold or copy reads `needsUserAction`.
- **Holds** (`content/report.ts` `lastFillOf`): Next mode Auto is held by required fields left empty (`flagged`, a required field the run skipped among them) and hard-rule stops (`blocked`) alone; `unresolved` is always 0; an entry left to add holds neither Next nor Submit Auto (the `entries-left` refusal is never given).
- **Consents** (D5; `core/resolver/resolve.ts` `DECIDED_CONSENT_CAUSES`, 'binding-waiver' and 'description-only'; `policy.ts` `ConsentContext.decided`, `consentValue`): a consent the rules hand over for one of those causes goes to `consentValue` instead. 'description-only' (a consent read from its help text alone) is decided by its kinds like any consent (the v4 review, security M3): under All it is approved only when every kind is one the extension recognises, so an optional binding clause found only in help text is `consent-optional-unticked` and a required one `consent.required`; 'binding-waiver' is never approved, so `consent.required` agrees to it where the form requires it, under either mode, and an optional one is `consent-optional-unticked`. A binding waiver asked as a Yes/No is decided only when its sole ask is to take something on from now on (`labelRules.ts` `asksToAgree`, the v4 review, security M2): a fact (`COMMITMENT_FACT`), a "that"-clause (`AGREE_THAT`: "I acknowledge that I am not bound by …"), a first- or second-person state (`COMMITMENT_STATE`: "I am / you are / I have (not) … bound, subject, party to, signed, under, entered into") or any denying word (`DENIES`: "not", "no", "never" …) makes it a statement the run cannot know, left unanswered (`consent-requires-user`), as is a question about how the application was made.
- **Notices** (D9; `content/report.ts` `DROPPED_NOTICES`): the dropped codes are never raised; those the worker reads stay in `FrameReport.notices` as facts (the agent's verdict, `saysNoAi`, the counters), drawn by neither the page nor the side panel.

### Toasts in place of the notices (`src/content/ui/toasts.ts`)
`content/ui/notices.ts` is gone. `page-ui.ts` mounts the toasts in the closed root: in the top frame fixed at the viewport's top-right (`TOAST_INSET_PX`, 16, and `EDGE_CLEARANCE_PX` left of the launcher's tab while it shows), moved into the in-page panel's column, above it, while that is open; in an embedded frame, in the anchor's column under the launcher. One toast per kind (`TOAST_KINDS`: challenge, sign-in, no-sign-in, submit-held, submit-errors, validation, tripwire, complete), each led by TailorIT's mark and name; its × hides it until its kind leaves or its sentence changes; trusted clicks only, each counted only after `CONTROL_SEEN_MS`. `PageUi.setToasts(list)`, `PageUi.toasts` (tests); the orchestrator recomputes them (`drawToasts`, `toastsNow`) at each change of the run. The no-sign-in toast's Open opens TailorIT's `/settings/autofill` in a new tab (`window.open`, `noopener`) inside the person's click. A toast's sentence carries the `.notice` class, so trap spec 20 still reads the tripwire's on the page.
- **What a sign-in reports** (`content/sign-in.ts` `SignInOutcome`; the v4 review, M5): `none-saved` (the worker's `not_found`, "The account wall" below) and `challenged` (a challenge shows, so nothing was asked, typed or pressed) apart from `unavailable`. The toasts follow, in the run (`toastsNow`) and at a wall with no form (`showWallToast`): `none-saved` the no-sign-in toast with its Open; `challenged` the challenge toast; `failed` the sign-in toast with the "didn't work" words; anything else the sign-in toast. The agent's entry reads `challenged` as block `captcha`.
- **The challenge check comes first** (`content/sign-in.ts`; the v4 review, L5): a challenge shown and not solved is checked before anything else, the prelude press included, so a checkbox CAPTCHA (no pause for `detectPause`) keeps Workday's "Sign in with email" unpressed. The prelude door `dom/fillers/not-submit.ts` `clickAdapterSignInPrelude` refuses by Sign-in's own rule too: an unsolved widget, a vendor's frame, or a frame that reads like a challenge.
- **The complete toast** says "required fields" only when the progress counts required fields (`progressOf`'s `requiredOnly`; the v4 review, L8), else "fields", as the side panel's line.
- **Auto-resume** (`orchestrator.ts` `watchPauseGone`): a paused run looks for its pause again `PAUSE_LOOK_MS` (250 ms) after each DOM change, for `PAUSE_WATCH_MS` (30 minutes) at most, and continues once it is gone (`runtime.ts`, at most `MAX_AUTO_CONTINUES`, 20, a document), a sign-in wall included. The v4 review (behaviour H2): the pause it rests at is the machine's (`restingKinds`): auth-needed means the account walls (sign-in, create-account, email gate), handoff its reason where the page can show one (CAPTCHA, email code, entry choice, password reset). Where the page no longer shows it (a run `resumeStored` restored in the document the person's sign-in or solved check brought), the run carries on at once. A pause no page signal names (a page busy, a Next not taken, a bot check asked as a question) is compared with what the page showed at rest, as before. Sign-in Auto is due only while the page shows an account wall now, and one a challenge held back (`signInSaid` `'challenged'`) is tried again once the page shows no challenge by Sign-in's rule.
- **The launcher's badge** (D11; `content/ui/launcher.ts` `badgeOf`): the percent of the required fields filled (of every field when none is required: `progressOf`), brand tone, a tick at 100 % or when nothing counts, "!" only for not connected or failed. Its review tooltip (`launcherReview`, `7623a90`) reads "Autofill is done: N% filled. Open the review", true either way (it said "of the required fields").

### The account wall and Sign up & Autofill
- **`frame/wall {wall: SignInWall | null, choice?: true}`** (`messaging/schemas.ts`): sent by a tab's top frame or claim frame whenever what it shows changes (`runtime.ts` `watchWall`, `tellWall`: `detectPause` with the page's adapter, or the address's own where there is no form; a look `WALL_LOOK_MS` after each DOM change; never while a run of the frame works). The worker keeps it on the frame's registry entry (`frames.ts` `setWall`, kept across the same document's hellos), and `pageOfTab` sets `PageState.wall` from `wallFrameOf` (the claim frame, else the top frame).
- **Sign up & Autofill** (`background/sign-up.ts`): `panel/fill {signIn: true}` (never a dry run) grants the tab Sign-in Auto for one run, in memory only (`SIGN_UP_GRANT_MS`, 10 minutes; ended by the tab's report at review, done or stopped, a plain `panel/fill`, the tab closing or the worker restarting). `contentSettingsOf(…, {signUp})` (through `index.ts` `contentSettingsFor`) says Sign-in 'auto' for that tab, pushed to its frames at once (`pushTab`), and `sign-in.ts` `signInAuto` answers the credential there, every other rule held. `run/start` carries `signIn: true`; the run (`RunRequest.signIn`, kept by its Continue) signs in at its auth-needed pause. Where no frame has a form but the application frame shows a wall or a choice, `run/start` goes to that frame (`runtime.ts` `atWall`), and the tab's next form that says hello is started once (`SignUps.takeFill`).
- **The grant keeps to its place** (the v4 review, security M1, behaviour M3): it records where it was given (`GrantPlace`): the origin of the tab's application frame (`wallFrameOf`: the claim frame, else the top frame; with neither of ours no grant is made) and that frame's application key where it has one.
  - Sign-in Auto reaches only frames of that origin: `contentSettingsFor` takes the frame's origin (the hello answer, `run/begin`, `pushTab`, `pushNow`, the agent's release), and `signInAuto(tabId, origin)` answers `signin/credential` only to a sender of that origin.
  - `takeFill` starts only the top frame, or the claim frame or one of its chain frames (`agentFramesOf`: iCIMS's IForm), on the grant's origin and at the same application when both name one; a wall with no application key (Workday's `/login`) lets the first form of that origin start within the grant's time, whatever its application (docs/OWNER-QUESTIONS.md 189).
  - It also ends when the tab's top frame says hello for a new document on another origin (`moved`), and at `SIGN_UP_GRANT_MS` by the worker's timer or a read after that time. An ending grant that held Sign-in Auto sends the tab's frames their settings again (`pushTab`: frames with a form, a wall or an entry choice), so they stop reading Sign-in 'auto', an end by time included. Not covered: a worker the browser stops loses its grants without telling the frames; that stale 'auto' is harmless, since every run reads its settings at `run/begin` and the worker refuses a wall's own sign-in, so the page shows the sign-in toast.
- **`signin/credential` refusals** (the v4 review, M5): `unavailable` (Sign-in Auto not allowed now: no `credentials:read`, the disclosure not acknowledged, Sign-in Manual and no grant for the sender's origin; or TailorIT not reached or refusing); `not_found`, a new code of `messaging/envelope.ts` `ERROR_CODES` (TailorIT holds no sign-in this page may use: it answered 404, or a sign-in for another host, or the every-site `*` on a page `*` does not cover); `expired`, `bad_sender` and `rate_limited` as before. Only `not_found` makes the page say "Add your job-site sign-in in TailorIT".
- **Workday's entry choice** (D7): Autofill at the choice page, which shows no form, presses the adapter's declared entry (`press.ts` `pressEntry`: Apply Manually); `frame/wall {choice}` tells the worker the page shows one. **`PageState.choice`** (`frames.ts` `pageOfTab`; the v4 review, M4): set on a page with no form, a posting page included (Workday's `/apply`, which its parser reads as the posting), while the tab's application frame (`wallFrameOf`) said `frame/wall {choice: true}`; the next `frame/wall` without it, or a new document, clears it. The side panel then offers Autofill there ("The side panel's views" above).

### The steps machine and settings version 6
- **The machine** (`core/steps/machine.ts`): `TIMED_OUT` no longer pauses (the step is validated again with its lastFill cleared, or the new one scanned); `NEXT_WITHHELD` takes `not-pressable` (the run rests for the person's Next); a page still busy at the settle cap has its step validated as it stands; no `policy-notice` transition, and a stored `policy-notice` run rescans on `RESUMED`. `run/policyDecision` is answered `expired`; `panel/policyDecision` stays in the catalogue, sent by no panel.
- **The run goes on** (`runtime.ts` `continueRun`; the v4 review, behaviour H1): a `run/start` that is not a dry run, reaching a run that rests offering Continue (`ended === 'paused'`: a page turned under Autofill after page turn: Manually, a pause, a run offered after a reload), continues that run as the launcher's Continue does: its RunState, canaries, step attempts, `MAX_STEPS` count and what it carries are kept. It is Sign up & Autofill only when this fill asks (`run/start {signIn}`): a plain Autofill, whose `panel/fill` ends the tab's grant, continues without it; the launcher's Continue and a pause that goes keep the run's own. A paused dry run is never continued by a fill, which starts afresh.
- **Settings version 6** (`core/types/settings.ts` `migrateSettingsV5`): `llm.whenForbidden` loses 'ask' (a stored 'ask' reads 'profile-only'), and `llm.confirmDrafts` goes (version 7 since snapshot round 2: "Snapshot round 2" below); `shared/settings-stored.ts` holds `SettingsV5Schema` and versions 2 to 5's `LlmV5`. The policy card's code (`FillRun`'s accept and decline, its card mid-fill) and the machine's `POLICY_ACCEPTED` and `POLICY_DECLINED` are gone. `offerToSaveAnswers` is still stored and offered nowhere.

### Typeable dropdowns: candidate answers
- **Candidate answers** (`core/matcher/candidates.ts` `searchCandidates(intended, vocab, place?, extras?)`; `core/matcher/searchLimits.ts`): the texts a search types, one at a time, from TailorIT's own data only (countries, regions, degrees, institutions, CIP fields), per kind, in order, none repeated (ignoring case and white space), each at least 3 characters (a code 2, `CODE_QUERY_MIN_LENGTH`: "BS", "+1"), at most 300, at most `MAX_SEARCH_QUERIES` (6; 4 since snapshot round 2, one candidate of each of three styles and then "Other": "Snapshot round 2" below):
  - a school: the person's text; the canonical name; without a leading "The"; curated acronyms ("MIT"); the other curated spellings ("UT Austin", "UTexas"); the name without its campus (`campusSplit`: before " at ", a spaced dash, a "-<campus>" the vocabulary names, or the last comma); the flagship's bare name; the vocabulary's other spellings; the two weighty words ("Texas Austin"). Nothing the vocabulary marks ambiguous;
  - a country (a phone country too): its name; the person's text; its other spellings; its alpha-3; and, only for a phone-country list whose options show a dial code, the person's dial code (`SearchExtras.dialCode`, which `shapeFor` passes);
  - a degree: the person's text; the type's name; its first code dotted and not ("B.S.", "BS"); the level's name without and with "degree";
  - a field of study: the person's text; the CIP title; its other spellings; the parent code's title;
  - any other entity: the person's text, the name, without "The", the other spellings, the weighty words;
  - a place (`Choice.place`): the profile's text, then "City, RG", "City, Region", "City, Country", "City";
  - any other text: as written, then without "The".

  `typeaheadQueries` is now `searchCandidates`: UT Austin gives six queries (four before; since snapshot round 2 three, then "Other"), "Austin, TX" four. Matching is unchanged: every query's results go to the worker and through `matchOne` against all the value's spellings, never "the first result that holds the text". The extra rule the brief allowed (a single result equal to, or beginning with, the candidate) was not added: no fixture or test called for it. `TYPEAHEAD_MAX_QUERIES` (4, `core/confidence.ts`) is gone; the cap is `MAX_SEARCH_QUERIES`.
- **The planner** (`core/resolver/resolve.ts` `shapeFor`, `listSearchFor`; `core/plan/frame.ts` `planChoice`, `searchInstead`, `fillsWithoutDoubt`): a combobox's `choice` shape gets a `search` list (its candidate answers) when the list is long (`SEARCH_LIST_MIN_OPTIONS`, 50, the same number as `WIDGET_SEARCH_MIN_OPTIONS`) or read only in part, and the value is the profile's. Where no option of the read is the value, such a combobox is planned as a `query` write of them, unless the widget already shows the value (another answer shown: searched, flagged `prefill-conflict`, as a remote search is) or the value's own confidence cannot reach the fill band. A search's results reach the worker marked `FieldSpec.searchResults` (`content/pass.ts` `resultsSpec`): never sent to the LLM tiebreak or TailorIT's AI answers (`plan/answers.ts` `choiceOptions`), and one is chosen only in the fill band (`fillsWithoutDoubt`), whatever `confidence.ts` holds. The first result in the fill band ends the search.
- **The frame** (`content/pass.ts` `searchField`; `dom/widgets`): a combobox's `query` write is searched as a remote typeahead's (`searchTypeahead`: each query a guarded write, transform `typeahead.query`), only where `searchesList` holds (declared by its adapter, `aria-autocomplete` list or both, long or read in part; moved to `dom/widgets/aria.ts`, still exported from `dom/widgets`); any other list is left not written (`options-incomplete` or `no-match`), its box untouched. `searchTypeahead` types at most `MAX_SEARCH_QUERIES` and stops before a query once the field has searched for `SEARCH_FIELD_BUDGET_MS` (15 s, `dom/thresholds.ts`). With nothing chosen, the box gets back what it held before the first query and its list is closed, as before, but for a widget whose adapter declares its results list (`WidgetDecl.results`, today Lever's alone) and whose box held text (`typeahead.ts` `keepsListOpen`; the v4 review): its text is put back with the setter, input and change events and no focusout or blur, and its list is left open (Lever's below). An empty box is restored and closed as before. `widgetHolds` reads back a long list's option chosen among one query's results by what the widget shows, with no known options (its place in the read is unknown: Greenhouse's phone-country reading would otherwise map a "+1" to the only +1 country of a partial read).
- **A declared results list** (`adapters/types.ts` `WidgetDecl.results {list, option, choose?}`, `WidgetDecl.queryKey`): a search's results list no ARIA names, from a capture: the element holding the results, a selector for one, and the one event that chooses one where `click()` does not (`'mousedown'`). `isActionable` accepts a declared `typeahead-remote` with a text box and such a list; `widgetListOf` gives the list (else ARIA's), `optionElementsOf(list, selector)` its results, and every list helper (open, wait, read, reveal, close, chips) reads it. `queryKey`: a declared key sent (keydown and keyup, untrusted, through `assertKeyTarget`) to the text box after each typed query, for a search that starts on a keydown and never on the input event, only once the page has answered typed text itself; a page whose script does not run (the static Lever snapshots of e2e 21 and 80) gets no key and stops at its first query as needs-your-click.
- **Lever's Current location** (`adapters/lever/index.ts`): `results` is `div.dropdown-results`, option `.dropdown-location`, `choose: 'mousedown'`; `queryKey: 'Escape'`; busy sign `.dropdown-loading-results`. Its `labelFor` names the box by the question's own words, since the results and "No location found" show inside its wrapping `<label>` (a fresh scan between queries would otherwise give the field another label and key). The hidden `selectedLocation` input is never written: the page fills it from the chosen result. The public page's own script was read for behaviour only: an input event shows the list; a keydown of any key but the arrows and Enter starts the search 500 ms after the last one; a mousedown on a result chooses it; a blur while the list shows empties the box. Escape is the declared key because it starts the search there and does nothing else. Since that script empties the box and its hidden `selectedLocation` on a blur while its list shows, and closes the list only when a result is chosen, a search that finds nothing leaves the box with the place it held (words and record) and the list ("No location found", or results that don't match) open under it (`keepsListOpen`, above).

### Tests
`src/ui/panel/views.browser.test.tsx`, `src/ui/page/helptip.browser.test.tsx`; `src/background/profile-check.test.ts`, `letter-preview.test.ts`, `sign-up-worker.test.ts`; `src/content/sign-in.browser.test.ts` (the wall, its toast, Sign up & Autofill), `src/content/report.test.ts` (D3, D8, D9); `src/core/matcher/candidates.test.ts`, `src/content/search.browser.test.ts`, `src/adapters/lever/location.browser.test.ts`; e2e `28-remote-typeahead` (six queries), `30-policy` (no card: profile only by default, or "Use AI anyway") and `48-v4-lever-location`. The fixtures sweeps of `test/mhtml-to-fixture.test.ts` skip the git-ignored `fixtures/_incoming/` and `fixtures/.generated/` (docs/FIXTURES.md). The v4 review's fixes add cases to `src/background/sign-up-worker.test.ts` (the grant's origin and application, its ends, `PageState.choice`), `sign-in.test.ts` and `agent-worker.test.ts`; `src/content/sign-in.browser.test.ts`, `workday-sign-in.browser.test.ts`, `complete-toast.browser.test.ts`, `steps.browser.test.ts`, `phase6.browser.test.ts` and `report.test.ts`; `src/dom/fillers/sign-in-doors.browser.test.ts`; `src/core/resolver/policy.test.ts`, `src/core/plan/person-only.test.ts`; `src/shared/progress.test.ts`; `src/adapters/lever/location.browser.test.ts`; and the side panel's `src/ui/panel/panel.browser.test.tsx` and `views.browser.test.tsx`, `src/ui/report/model.test.ts` and `report.browser.test.tsx` (tests not about the hold itself use `ui/ui.testkit.ts` `SEEN_AT_ONCE`). The e2e helper's `fill`: "Testing", "How tests act as the person".

## Snapshot round 2: the owner's requests of 2026-11-07, evening (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §14)
What follows holds over every note above and below where they differ. Shared shapes `c2c3dff` (the optional profile values, `modes.noMatch`, `modes.notifications`); five builds merged (`2c32cd3` Workday, `de59e04` Greenhouse and Lever questions, `192099a` three styles and My Information, `9db0b80` profile and settings, `979a3b3` lists); the reviews' fixes `aa4e501` and `fae55a4` (C1 to C10), folded in below. Each rule names the snapshot or fixture it came from (docs/FIXTURES.md "The owner's snapshots of 2026-11-07"). **Pending** (being built, updated here when merged): the stored lists served by the worker before a step's scan, for the content script's 1 MiB budget (`scripts/check-build.mjs` `CONTENT_SCRIPT_BUDGET`; the lists builder measured 1,057,351 bytes at `979a3b3`), the review's L1 and L2 (learned lists) and P1 (the side panel's waits under timed notifications). Builders mark their code `// s2 <group>`.

### Stored lists, chosen at once (decision 1)
- **The declaration** (`adapters/types.ts`): `WidgetDecl.knownOptions` is a combobox's whole list as its adapter stores it from the snapshots, every label as a whole read takes it (Workday's "Select One" first), in the list's order; `WidgetDecl.drawnWhole` says the page draws every option at once, so `readList` and `revealOption` scroll without waiting for rows to be drawn (61 states read at once; 250 countries took 6.4 s view by view).
- **The scan** (`dom/scan.ts` `withReading`): a combobox with no reading this run and its list not shown gets its stored list as its options, complete, so the planner plans it on pass 1 and the list is never opened to be read; a reading (the waiting path's, a fill's whole read) or a list shown now always comes first. `WidgetTarget.knownOptions` and `knownFrom` (`'adapter'` or `'learned'`) are set only while the options are that list; a placeholder ("Select One") is a disabled option.
- **The choice** (`dom/widgets/fill.ts` `chooseKnown`, from `chooseByEvents`): the list opened once, the planned option found at its place by its label as the page shows it (`dom/widgets/known.ts` `findKnown`, one query, the list scrolled to it), clicked through `assertNotSubmit`, the list closed, read back as before. A list that holds no such option there is read whole and kept as the field's reading (and learned, below), nothing chosen: `options-changed`, and the next pass plans from what the page showed. An llm-pick is still checked on a whole read (R1). React first stays first: a Greenhouse react-select with a stored list goes to the bridge by its label, then the search, then the choice at once.
- **The lists:** Workday (`adapters/workday/lists.ts`, `storedListOf`; `application.ts` `widgetFor`): every dropdown is `drawnWhole`; My Information's Country (`formField-country > button[name=country]`) holds `WORKDAY_COUNTRIES` (249, snapshot1 exelixis), the State (`formField-countryRegion > button[name=countryRegion]`) `WORKDAY_US_STATES` (60, `s2-exelixis-state-open`) only while the step's one Country shows `WORKDAY_US` chosen. Greenhouse (`adapters/greenhouse/lists.ts`): the phone's country (`fieldset.phone-input` `#country`) `GREENHOUSE_PHONE_COUNTRIES` (244 "<Name> +<dial>", "United States +1" first, the pairs of `src/core/matcher/phone-countries.fixture.json`, `rushdown-country-open` and snapshot3's Pie list), drawn whole; the EEOC block's `#gender`, `#hispanic_ethnicity` and `#veteran_status` (`div.eeoc__container`) `EEOC_GENDER`, `EEOC_HISPANIC`, `EEOC_VETERAN` (`s2-pie-eeo-6`, `-7`, `-8`). Race and `disability_status` are in no open list: read as before. No Workday bridge op.
- **React before React attaches:** a stored list's react-select is planned on pass 1, so its bridge command may be the run's first contact with a page whose React has not attached (`no-react`): `content/pass.ts` `writeWidget` then takes the run's one wait for the page (`dom/widgets/interact.ts` `awaitPageReady`), guards the field again (`domGuardBeforeWrite`, `guardWriteInFrame`) and sends the command once more (`BridgeAttempt` carries the bridge's `reason`). Found by e2e 33's "React attaches late".
- **Pending (the content script's size budget):** the labels move into the worker, which serves them before a step's scan; the adapters then name a list by id and an expectation reads such a widget `"lazy"` again (docs/FIXTURES.md).

### Learned lists (decision 1)
- **What is learnable** (`dom/widgets/known.ts` `learnable`, `learnedLabels`, `learnedListKey`): a select-only dropdown its adapter draws whole and stores no list for (Workday's Degree, Phone Device Type, Prefix, Suffix, an Application Questions dropdown). A complete whole read of one (the waiting path's or a fill's) is told to the run (`WidgetFillContext.learn`), which keeps it for the rest of the run under its label's key (another block's dropdown of that label plans from it) and sends `lists/learn` once. The orchestrator asks `lists/known` once a step's scan holds a learnable dropdown; every later scan gives each such dropdown its label's list (`ScanOptions.learnedLists`), kept honest by the open-and-check. A dropdown with a stored list of its own never takes a learned one.
- **The worker** (`background/learned-lists.ts`, `shared/learned-lists.ts`): `storage.local` `lists:v1`; per list the first 16 hex digits of the SHA-256 of the frame's host (Chrome's sender address, never a payload) and of the label slug, `optionSetHash` of the options, the labels (`MAX_LEARNED_OPTIONS` 300, `MAX_LEARNED_LABEL` 160) and when; `MAX_LEARNED_LISTS` 200 across hosts (the least recently learned go first), `MAX_LEARNED_PER_HOST` 60 offered; writes queued one at a time; nothing kept from a private tab (`lists.private` logged), its run still offered the host's lists. **Pending:** the review's L1 (`lists:v2`, the site an HMAC under a per-install secret, no time, a byte budget and a per-site cap, `wd<n>.myworkdaysite.com` keyed by its tenant path or never kept) and L2 (a dependent list keyed with the value of the field it depends on, Workday's State with its Country; a learned list the plan found no option in dropped once and read again).

### Greenhouse's phone (decision 2)
- The country inside `fieldset.phone-input` is read by its own label first (`labelFirst`), with the adapter's `labelSuffix` "code": "Country code", so the core classifies it `contact.phoneCountry` (the phone's own country), never `address.country`; the fieldset's legend "Phone" is visually hidden.
- With its stored list it is planned on pass 1 with the number; `separatePhoneCountry` (`core/plan/frame.ts`) types the number without its dial code, and `content/phone-order.ts` `phoneCountryFirst` (the loop's queue) writes each section's phone country before its number whatever order the reveal edges gave.
- A number the page empties after the country's choice is a value the page reset: the next pass types it again (`core/loop/bookkeeping.ts` `'value-changed'`, bounded by `MAX_VALUE_FLIPS`). No new code: proven with `MountOptions.phoneClearsMs` on the synthetic phone form (`src/content/greenhouse-phone.browser.test.ts`).

### Workday: dates (decision 3)
- **The field** (`Adapter.dateFor`, `adapters/types.ts` `DatePartsDecl`): each `div[role=group][data-automation-id=dateInputWrapper]` inside a `formField-*` is one field of kind `date`, its parts (`input[role=spinbutton][data-automation-id=dateSection{Month,Day,Year}-input]`, each with its `-display`) in page order, `keys: 'digits'`. The scanner (`dom/scan.ts`) makes the wrapper the control (`FieldTarget.dateParts`), its format from its parts (month+year `month-year`, a year `year`, month+day+year `date`), required, disabled and read-only from the parts, its value from the displays (`dom/fillers/date-parts.ts` `datePartsValue`: "2025-04" when every part shows digits, empty when none); its label is its fieldset's legend. The parts stay hidden controls the scan ignores.
- **The write** (`fillDateParts`, awaited by `content/pass.ts` `writeField`): each part's own digits through the value setter with input, change, focusout and blur; one quiet window (`WIDGET_QUIET_MS`, capped at `WIDGET_CAP_MS`); read back from the displays and `aria-valuenow`; a part not taken gets its own value back, then its digits as declared keys (one keydown and keyup per digit, through `assertKeyTarget`, that part only), read back again. Verified, written not verified, or nothing taken (each part given back, `needs-your-click`). Never a made-up day (a month and year into three parts: `format-unsupported`); the calendar (`dateIcon`) never pressed. The DOM guard asks about the parts; readBack, `holds` and `markedInvalid` read them; `fill()` refuses a date-parts target. A current job's To stays unwritten (`planCurrentJob`; `content/repeatables.ts` reads a parts end date from its displays).
- **Next** (`requiredDateEmpty`): withheld while a visible date has a required part whose display or `aria-valuenow` shows no digits, as a person reads it, not by the hidden input's value. This replaces "Dates" under "Workday's application steps" above.

### Workday: prompts and declared results lists (decision 4)
- `widgetFor` declares each prompt (School, Field of Study, Skills, How Did You Hear About Us, Country Phone Code) with `results: {list: () => promptListOf(container), option: PROMPT_ROW, choose: 'label', unchoosable: UNCHOOSABLE_ROW}`, `optionLabel` its `promptOption`, `queryKey: 'Enter'`. `promptListOf` finds the `activeListContainer` of the `responsiveMonikerPrompt` whose `data-associated-widget` is the prompt container's id (drawn at the end of the body only while open). `UNCHOOSABLE_ROW`: a category (a chevron, no radio: `s2-verily-source-open`) and the list's own "No Items." row (`s2-verily-school-no-items`), read as unavailable results, never chosen. A result is chosen by `click()` on its `promptOption`. This replaces "the helpers never type into it" under "Workday's application steps" above.
- **Widget results** (`dom/widgets`): `WidgetResults.list` may be a hook (`aria.ts` `widgetListOf`, found anew each time; one that throws finds none); `unchoosable` read as disabled by `options.ts` `shownOptions` and `readList`; `choose: 'label'` (`typeahead.ts` `pressTargetOf`: both the row and its part pass `assertNotSubmit`). `typeWatched` also sees an answer the page draws in the microtasks the input event queued (React), so a declared query key follows the first query on a React page too; still no key to a page that answers nothing.
- **Keys** (`dom/fillers/events.ts`): `WIDGET_KEYS` (Escape, ArrowDown: `WidgetDecl.keys`, `Adapter.keyAroundWrite`), `QUERY_KEYS` (those and Enter: `WidgetDecl.queryKey`), `DIGIT_KEYS` (a date's parts), `DECLARED_KEYS` all of them; Enter and the digits carry legacy `keyCode`/`which` (13, 48 to 57); `KEY_EVENTS` still keydown and keyup.

### Workday: My Information and My Experience (decision 6)
- **Repeatables:** `repeatables()`'s `blocks()` and `addButton()` look the section up again on each call (a section redrawn after an Add is read). `core/steps/repeatable.ts` `planAdd({newDocument})`: the first plan of a section in a new document that shows fewer blocks than were counted starts the Add count again (a reload dropped unsaved blocks); `content/repeatables.ts` `addBlocks` passes it.
- **New categories** (`types/intent.ts`, `categories.ts`, class profile): `identity.suffix`, `contact.phoneExtension`, `identity.hasPreferredName`; `contact.phoneType` now has a rule and a value; `contact.phoneCountry` and `referral.source` also take `typeahead-remote` (Workday's prompts). `labelRules.ts` `WORKDAY_INFO_RULES` (before §8.3's list, on the label's own noun): `label.phone-type`, `label.suffix`, `label.phone-extension`, `label.has-preferred-name`; `label.phone-country` also reads "country phone code"; `label.location` inside an experience block (not "current") is `experience.location`; `EXPERIENCE_DESCRIPTION` takes "role/job/position description"; a GPA asked in a parenthetical ("Overall Result (GPA)") is `education.gpa`. `nameId.ts` `name-id.twitter` classifies Workday's X box (`name=twitterAccount`).
- **Values** (`values.ts`): the phone's type by its usual spellings, none stored decided "Mobile" (`VALUE_DERIVED`, filled and marked); a suffix as written and in its dotted, undotted and spelled-out forms; the extension; "I have a preferred name" only for a preferred name that differs from the first name. With Country Phone Code classified, `separatePhoneCountry` holds in Workday's phone section, so the number is typed nationally.

### Three styles, then Other or leave (decision 5)
- **Styles** (`core/matcher/candidates.ts` `SEARCH_STYLES`: `full`, `short`, `reduced`; `searchLimits.ts`): a search types the first candidate of each style, at most `MAX_STYLE_QUERIES` (3), in the candidates' order, an unused candidate taking a style that has none; the person's own text takes the style of its spelling ("UT Austin" is short, so the full name is still typed). A school: full the person's text or the canonical name without a leading "The" (then with it), short the curated acronyms and spellings, reduced `campusSplit`, the flagship's bare name, the weighty words. A country (a phone country too): the name, then other spellings and the alpha-3, then the dial code (`SearchExtras.dialCode`: a list showing dial codes, and now Workday's Country Phone Code prompt, `resolve.ts` `remoteSearchExtras`). A degree: its text or type, its codes, the level. A field of study: its text or CIP title, its spellings, the parent title. A referral source: its text or name, its spellings, its kind (`SearchExtras.kind`). A place: the profile's text, "City, RG" and the like, the city alone. Any other text: as written, then without "The".
- **Limits:** `MAX_SEARCH_QUERIES` is 4 (`MAX_STYLE_QUERIES` + 1; was 6 since v4), used by the message schema's query cap and the DOM's `queries.slice`; `NO_MATCH_OTHER_QUERY` 'Other'.
- **The mode** (`core/plan/noMatch.ts` `noMatchOtherAllowed`): `settings.modes.noMatch` 'other', a category of `NO_MATCH_OTHER_CATEGORIES` (education.school, education.field, experience.company, experience.currentCompany, location.current, experience.location, referral.source, contact.phoneCountry), policy class profile and the value's provenance the profile: never EEO, legal, consent or yes/no, nor an AI, saved or rule answer.
- **The planner** (`core/plan/frame.ts`): `queryValue` puts `other: true` on a query write (a remote typeahead's and a long list's `searchInstead`); `planNoMatchOther` takes results marked `FieldSpec.searchOther`, checks the mode and the value's fill band again, and chooses the one strict "Other" (`core/matcher/other.ts` `noMatchOtherMatch`: the whole label folded by `foldOtherLabel` one of `NO_MATCH_OTHER_LABELS`; never two, a disabled one or one with injected text), stage 'other', rule `other-free-text`, at `MATCH_NO_MATCH_OTHER` (0.75, always the flag band), reason `other-option-with-free-text`, its free text the person's own, which `content/follow-up.ts` types into a revealed "please specify" box. `planField` lets a typeahead-remote take that flag-band choice alone. Under 'leave', stage e's "Other" on a list read whole is left unchosen too.
- **The frame** (`content/pass.ts` `searchField`): when the styles chose nothing, `otherQueryFollows` types "Other" once more (the write says `other`, no Guard refused, the reason none of `OTHER_QUERY_STOPS`, the run goes on, `SEARCH_FIELD_BUDGET_MS` not spent), through the same guarded `searchTypeahead` on a fresh target, its results sent as `resultsSpec(…, other = true)`; finding nothing, the styles' reason stands. A chosen option carrying free text is read back from its own live elements, with no rescan, so its revealed box is named its follow-up (a gap for any search's "Other" before).
- **A referral source's kind** (`Choice.fallback`, `values.ts` `referralKind`): the profile's `referralSource.category`, else the vocabulary's first parent (LinkedIn: job board); `planChoice` tries it, before any LLM tiebreak, where no option is the source itself; a search types it as its reduced style.

### Greenhouse and Lever questions (decisions 7 to 13, with the review's C1 to C9)
- **Wrong values** (decision 13): `labelRules.ts` `NEED_TO_SPONSOR` turns `label.work-auth` away from a need for the employer to sponsor (OneStream), so `label.sponsorship` reads it; the review (C1): that `unless` (with `SPONSORED_NEED`) reads only the label's question sentences (`Refinement.unlessQuestionsOnly`, `LabelContext.questions`), so "Are you authorized …? Candidates requiring sponsorship are not eligible." is authorization. `PreRule.questionsOnly` (`classifyRules.ts` `questionsOf`): `label.authorization-and-sponsorship` reads the label up to its last "?" when a statement follows (Ridgeline). `classifyRules.ts` `OTHER_PERSON_LABEL` reads the possessive ("referring employee's") and "the name of your relative"; `plan/frame.ts` `withoutOwnDetail` refuses an LLM classification to a profile category on a field that names another person (`namesAnotherPerson`) or on a follow-up (`conditionOf`).
- **The N/A rule** (decision 8; `values.ts` `placeholderAsked`, `resolve.ts`): a text box or textarea of a profile category the profile holds nothing for, whose own visible label or help text tells an applicant without one to type a placeholder, gets `NO_VALUE_PLACEHOLDERS` (N/A, NA, None; `types/intent.ts`, the page's token only selecting), rule `placeholder.no-profile`, required or optional; unclassified fields go to classification and the AI. The review (C9): `guard/provenance.ts` `checkProvenanceShape` refuses any other text under that rule.
- **Links and handles** (decision 9): links take a line or a box of text (`categories.ts` `PROSE`: OneStream's "LinkedIn Profile" textarea). X is `links.twitter` (`label.twitter` also reads "X username/handle/profile/url", never a bare X), Telegram `links.telegram` (`label.telegram`). `values.ts` `handleLinkValue`: the profile's `links.x`/`links.telegram`, else the one `links.other` link on that host; a box saying username, handle or "@" (and no URL or link) gets the bare handle (`link.handle`), "@handle" only where it shows "@". The review (C6, `handleLinkOf`): a box naming both takes Telegram, then X, then the N/A.
- **Consents and answers from the profile** (decisions 10, 11): a consent asked as a Yes and a No (`classifyRules.ts` `offersYesAndNo`) is decided by the Consents mode; "are you okay doing this (CLEAR ID) validation?" is `label.consent-check`. `links.website` falls back to the portfolio (`links.website-from-portfolio`). Authorization and sponsorship in a text box asked as yes/no (`YES_NO_QUESTION`) are typed "Yes"/"No" (`policy.ts` `asTyped`, `legal.yes-no-text`). A nationality asked by country is `workAuth.citizenship` (`label.citizenship-country`) from `screening.citizenships`.
- **EEO** (decision 12; `policy.ts` `eeoValue`, `plan/frame.ts`): Hispanic Yes adds the list's Hispanic or Latino option to a race multi and chooses it on a single list (`eeo.hispanic-race`); a list that splits Asian takes `raceSubgroups`; a multi takes every option the answers match (the CORE-4 whole-answer rule now for legal and sensitive sets only); an answer the list does not offer is its decline option (`eeo.not-offered`); sexual orientation, transgender, `veteran.activeDuty` and `raceSelfDescribed` answer from the profile. The review (C8): `classify.ts` `EXTENSION_ORIENTATION_TERMS` join the extension's own sensitive-topic screen.
- **New categories** (`labelRules.ts` `S2_RULES`, `categories.ts`): `address.full` (`label.full-address`, composed; the review, C7: `plan/frame.ts` `streetWhereSplit` makes it `address.line1` where its section has a city or postal-code box), `screening.restrictiveAgreement` (a non-compete asked as a fact, before the binding-waiver reading), `screening.governmentService`, `screening.relatives`, `screening.essentialFunctions`, `skills.certifications`, `preferences.relocationPlaces`, `education.summary`; `workAuth.clearance` reads `CLEARANCE_WIDE`. The screening ones and the citizenships are legal (explicit answers); `screening.governmentService` and `workAuth.clearance` are AI-opened where the profile holds none. The review: `policy.ts` `clearanceValue` answers from the stored level only a clearance held now (`CLEARANCE_HELD`; `CLEARANCE_NOT_HELD` or neither: not in the profile) (C2); `governmentServiceValue` reads "currently" as no end year, `WITHIN_YEARS` with or without "the last", no answer for an unreadable `TIME_LIMIT` (C3), and `GOVERNMENT_TRADE` turns the rule away (C5); `skills.certifications` takes every stored certification for the generic question, those matching the topic otherwise, none as no profile answer, and is in `ANSWERABLE_PROFILE_CATEGORIES` again (C4). `label.previously-employed`, `label.location` (a residence asked as a question) and `label.skills-used` read more wordings; a right-to-work list ("I have the right to work" / "I require sponsorship") chooses by the explicit authorization.
- **Provenance** (`guard/provenance.ts`): rules `placeholder.no-profile`, `link.handle`, `links.website-from-portfolio`, `legal.yes-no-text`, `eeo.hispanic-race`, `eeo.not-offered`, `compose.text`, each scoped; `PROFILE_PATH_PATTERN` gains `certifications`.

### AI answers that arrive late (decision 14)
- **Root cause** (reproduced in `src/adapters/greenhouse/ai-late.browser.test.ts`): the worker runs one AI task at a time per run (`background/ai-tasks.ts` `createRunAi`), so answers come batch after batch, each taking a pass; the loop ended at `MAX_PASSES` (8) with fields still awaiting (`max-passes-reached`), the AI-only react-selects last in the chain.
- **The fix:** `core/loop/bookkeeping.ts` `MAX_AI_PASSES` (20): past `MAX_PASSES` the loop goes on while a field awaits the AI, a list waits for options, or a list just read was rowed waiting for them; `content/loop.ts` `AI_WAIT_CAP_MS` (125 s with no answer coming) still ends a wait; page churn alone still ends at 8. `plan/frame.ts` `readFirst`: a lazy list nothing classified is rowed `waiting-for-options`, read, then classified once with its options. Unchanged: the one-task chain, its 120 s task cap, the 25 s POST timeout. An empty AI field's row reason says which: `max-passes-reached`, `llm-unavailable`, `ai-off-for-posting`, `llm-no-answer`.

### Profile values, settings version 7 and notification time (decisions 15, 16)
- **The contract** (TailorIT `v1.2-p11` `9318641`; `src/contract/tailorit/SOURCE.json`, autofill API still 6, 349 examples): new optional values with defaults; examples `snapshot-round-two.json` (profile and sensitive), invalid `unknown-phone-type.json` and `unknown-clearance-level.json`; the golden examples unchanged. TailorIT keeps a stored value a PUT omits (an older page or extension).
- **canonicalize** (`core/profile/canonicalize.ts`): `identity.suffix` and `contact.phone.extension` (in the contract since v1, mapped now), `contact.phone.type` (`PHONE_DEVICE_TYPES`), `links.x`/`telegram`, `preferences.relocationPlaces`, `certifications`, `referralSource.category` (`REFERRAL_CATEGORIES`; a kind with no source text reads as no answer); sensitive: `eeo.raceSubgroups` (`ASIAN_SUBGROUPS`, only beside Asian), `raceSelfDescribed`, `veteran.activeDuty`, `sexualOrientation` (`SEXUAL_ORIENTATIONS`), `transgender`, `screening.restrictiveAgreement`, `governmentService` (a year only beside its service; none means serving), `relativesAt`, `essentialFunctions`, `clearance` (`CLEARANCE_LEVELS`), `citizenships`. A value outside its list drops the answer whole with an issue (`value-unknown`, `eeo-value-unknown`) carrying a path, never the value.
- **Settings version 7** (`core/types/settings.ts`): `modes.noMatch` ('other' | 'leave') and `modes.notifications` ('until-closed' | '30s' | '10s' | '5s' | 'off'), defaults first in `MODE_CHOICES`, `NOTIFICATION_MS`; `migrateSettingsV6` (V5 to V1 through it) gives both their defaults; `shared/settings.ts` `ModesSchema`/`SettingsSchema` version 7, `ModesV6Schema`; `shared/settings-stored.ts` `SettingsV6Schema`; `background/settings.ts` logs `settings.migrated`. `ContentModesSchema` adds both with defaults; `contentSettingsOf` passes the person's own. `ui/page/modes.tsx` `SHOWN_MODES`: "When a list has no match" and "TailorIT notifications on the page" after Record applications I submit myself, on the options page and the Settings view. This replaces "settings version 6" above as the current version.
- **Toasts** (`content/ui/toasts.ts` `setTime`, `page-ui.ts` `setNotificationTime`, from `runtime.ts` at frame/hello and each `settings/changed`): a timed choice starts a `dom/time.ts` `startDeadline` per drawn toast, which leaves as if closed; 'off' draws none; the tripwire's (`ALWAYS_SHOWN`) always shows, until closed. Under 'off' the side panel (`Panel.tsx` → `ui/report/Report.tsx` `saysWaits`) adds a paused frame's reason (`tia-frame__note--pause`). **Pending (the review, P1):** the same under every timed choice, and a page with no run telling the worker what it waits for.
- **Editors:** TailorIT Settings › Autofill and the side panel's `ui/panel/Profile.tsx` edit the new values (the Asian subgroups only beside Asian). **Pending (the review, P2, P3, TailorIT):** its value screen compares the new word answers; a save keeps no subgroups without Asian.

### Messages, copy and tests
- **Message catalogue:** `lists/known` and `lists/learn` (below, "Content script → worker"); the error codes are unchanged. **Pending:** `lists/known` carries the stored lists' ids and returns their labels.
- **Copy:** `src/shared/copy.ts`, each group in its `// s2 <group>` block. `COPY.panel.unsupported.sites`' Workday note (`WORKDAY_STEPS`) now reads "Its application steps are filled, dates and search lists included."
- **Tests:** `src/adapters/greenhouse/stored-lists.browser.test.ts`, `src/adapters/workday/lists.browser.test.ts`, `src/content/learned-lists.browser.test.ts`, `src/background/learned-lists.test.ts`, `src/content/phone-order.test.ts`, `src/content/greenhouse-phone.browser.test.ts`; `src/adapters/workday/workday-s2.browser.test.ts`, `src/content/workday-dates.browser.test.ts`, `src/dom/widgets/keys.browser.test.ts`; `src/core/plan/noMatch.test.ts`, `src/core/resolver/workdayInfo.test.ts`, `src/content/no-match-other.browser.test.ts`, `src/adapters/workday/s2-core-wd.browser.test.ts`; `src/adapters/greenhouse/snapshots-2026-11-07.browser.test.ts`, `src/adapters/greenhouse/ai-late.browser.test.ts`; cases in the matcher, resolver, plan, guard, canonicalize, settings, toasts and panel tests; `test/mhtml-to-fixture.test.ts` (the converter's rules and the C10 sweep: docs/FIXTURES.md). e2e `28-remote-typeahead` (three styles, then "Other", four queries at most), `33-greenhouse-widgets` (the stored lists; its "React attaches late" case found the bridge's retry) and `34-ashby` (the school's first query without "The"). Each new rule was proven by a test that fails with it undone.

## Where the work happens
- **The extension:** a new git repository at `F:\Develop\TailorIT-autofill`, with no remote until the owner adds one; nothing is pushed.
  - One npm package, Node 24, exact dependency versions, a committed lockfile, and Windows-first Node scripts (TailorIT's conventions).
  - Its pure core cannot import `@tailorit/shared`; the few pure helpers it needs are rewritten there.
- **TailorIT:** the owner's checkout, `F:\Develop\TailorIT` (branch `v1.2`), on 3100 and 4100. Its autofill backend adds pairing, the profile, the job lookup, the resume file, the AI tasks, saved answers and the settings pages.
  - Phases 2-6 built it on the branch `autofill`, cut from `v1.1` in its own git worktree, `F:\Develop\TailorIT-wt-autofill`, with its own `npm install`. The owner's checkout stayed on v1.1 meanwhile, so their dev server (`tsx watch`, which migrates the database on every restart) never loaded an unreviewed migration.
  - The worktree had a git-ignored `.env`: `DATA_DIR=%LOCALAPPDATA%\TailorIT-autofill-dev`, web 3300, API 4300, and the fake Claude CLI.
  - After review the branch was fast-forwarded into the owner's checkout (`v1.2`, `65dc16e`, 2026-10-31), with their dev server stopped, and the worktree was retired. The scratch data folder stays.
- **The contract between them:** owned by TailorIT (`packages/shared/src/autofill/`, zod, `apiVersion: 3` since 2026-11-02; 1 at first), and copied byte for byte into the extension's `src/contract/tailorit/` by `npm run contract:pull -- F:\Develop\TailorIT`. Both repos test the copy against the same hashes and fixtures, so the two sides cannot drift silently.
- **Design reports:** the three reports written during planning (pure core, runtime, TailorIT's side) are saved in Phase 0 as `docs/design/{core,runtime,tailorit}.md`. They hold the full tables: rules, patterns, golden cases, DDL, routes. Where a report and this plan differ, the plan wins. They were corrected to agree with this file when they were saved.

## How it works at run time
```
ATS frame (top or embedded iframe)          extension service worker                  TailorIT (http://localhost:3100)
┌───────────────────────────────────────┐   ┌─────────────────────────────────────┐   ┌──────────────────────────────┐
│ content script (isolated world)       │   │ pure core: resolver, matcher, the   │   │ /api/autofill/* (token only) │
│  adapter, scanner, DOM Guard checks   │──►│  Guard's pure checks, planners      │──►│  profile, sensitive part,    │
│   (visibility, camouflage, tripwire)  │◄──│ profile, saved answers, vocabularies│◄──│  lookup, resume file,        │
│  filler + read-back, reactive loop,   │   │  (memory); token (IndexedDB);       │   │  AI pick/classify/draft      │
│  step machine; launcher, notices,     │   │  settings, RunState (storage)       │   │  (Claude CLI), saved answers │
│  in-page panel                        │   │ resume bytes → chunked base64       │   │                              │
└───────────────────────────────────────┘   └─────────────────────────────────────┘   └──────────────────────────────┘
    FieldSpec[] ──►   ◄── PlannedWrite[] (values for this frame's fields only)
    reports (no values) ──► side panel or in-page panel (review) ◄── the user
```
- **What the content script does:**
  - It scans its frame into `FieldSpec[]`: visible fields only, every label and option through `visibleText`.
  - It runs the DOM Guard checks, fills and reads back, settles, and runs the step machine's reducer (`reduceRun`).
  - It keeps every canary in memory for its own pre-write scan.
  - Its provenance re-check before each write is structural: the origin is present, the path pattern valid, the rule known, and an `llm-pick`'s option text unchanged.
- **What the worker does:**
  - It holds the profile and the saved answers, both fetched at `run/begin` and dropped together, plus the vocabularies (about 2 MB, parsed once).
  - It resolves and matches, runs the Guard's pure checks (including whether a profile path resolves), and answers `PlannedWrite[]`. So a frame never holds the whole profile, only the values for its own fields.
  - It submits the AI tasks.
  - It decides which canaries may be stored: its `runstate/put` handler drops every canary that equals a profile value. If the profile is not loaded at that moment, it loads it first; if that fails, it stores an empty `canarySet`. It never stores a canary it has not checked.
  - It enforces the RunState rules when storing, and derives the opt-in counters from report rows.
- **No install secret:**
  - An `llm-pick`'s option check uses an unkeyed SHA-256 of the clean option text as sent (page text, never stored).
  - Flip detection hashes values with a random per-run key held in memory only.
- **Starting a fill:** one click on the launcher or in the side panel fills the frame's form. The launcher accepts trusted clicks only (`event.isTrusted`), so a page can never start a fill.
- **Isolation:** the page cannot message the worker or start a fill. Nothing of the extension announces itself to a page: the content script is an unlisted script with no WXT content-script context, whose start-up event any page could listen for (the v3 Phase 6/7b review's round 2, E1). The forged WXT events (trap 11) are listened for only where the bridge installs, decided once, as the bridge's is, from the address the document was loaded at (`runtime.ts` `loadedHref`: its navigation entry, which no pushState of the page's moves), so elsewhere on an ATS domain no listener keyed to the extension's id runs for a page to time, nor does the page's own route to a posting address add one (round 3, X1, and round 4, Y1: following the route let a page push a posting address, time the listener and push back; e2e `00-smoke`, the debugger's listing of every world's listeners, after a pushState and with one made before the content script started). The content script says `frame/hello` only where the frame's address reads as a posting or its application form on its platform's candidate host (or the page after submitting: `src/adapters/address.ts`), so the worker never hears of an employer's own pages on the ATS domains the manifest matches (S3). The page-world bridge installs at document_start on any page of a host that serves only public job postings (`job-boards.greenhouse.io`, `boards.greenhouse.io`, `jobs.lever.co`, `jobs.eu.lever.co`, `jobs.ashbyhq.com`, `jobs.smartrecruiters.com`, `apply.workable.com`, `<tenant>[.wd<n>].myworkdayjobs.com`, and the new ATSs' candidate-only hosts: `jobs.jobvite.com`, `ats.rippling.com`, `recruiting.ultipro.com`, `jobs.dayforcehcm.com`, `<tenant>.careers.hibob.com`), so a board's client-side route to a posting keeps it (round 2, E9); on the domains that also hold an employer's own pages (iCIMS, Taleo, SuccessFactors, Oracle Cloud and its government cloud, Workday's shared `myworkdaysite.com`, `jobs.workable.com` and the new ATSs' path-scoped hosts) only where its address at document_start is a posting or its form, and nowhere else, not even as a silent listener a page could probe after a pushState to a posting-looking address (`bridgeInstallsAt`). There is no web-accessible resource, every message is zod-checked, and `externally_connectable` is limited to TailorIT's origin, for pairing only.
  - **The one page-world script** is the bridge (docs/SPEC-AMENDMENTS.md §12, the owner's "React first, like Jobright"): `src/entrypoints/page-bridge.content.ts`, a second content script with `world: 'MAIN'`, the same ATS hosts, every frame, from `document_start`, about 21 KB (20,921 bytes) with the shared domains' address parsers it installs by (`src/adapters/bridge-address.ts` `bridgeInstallsAt`, re-exported by `address.ts`: any page of a public job host, a posting or its form on a shared domain, read by `SHARED_DOMAIN_PARSERS` alone, the v3 Phase 6/7b review's S3 and its round 2's E9; check-build holds it to 24 KiB ("The page-world bridge's size" above) and reads its bundle: one anonymous function, naming no `chrome.`, `browser.` or `postMessage`, adding no global and listening for its command alone, and only there). It runs `src/page/bridge.ts`, which imports only its protocol (`src/dom/bridge-protocol.ts`; lint, which also forbids it the extension's and the page's globals, and `test/entrypoints.test.ts` keep it so): no zod, no React, no messaging, no storage, no browser API, no global. It holds no data and keeps no state but one capture listener on the document.
  - **The page fully controls the main world:** it can read every command, wrap every function the bridge calls, and forge commands and answers. So nothing may be sent there that the page should not read; later ops send no profile text there beyond what is being written into that same field at that moment. A page can also tell the extension is installed, from `document_start`, by the bridge's acknowledgement of a command.
  - **The protocol:** the content script (`src/dom/bridge.ts`) dispatches a non-bubbling, cancelable, composed `tailorit:bridge` CustomEvent on the element it is about, its detail a JSON string (a string crosses Chrome's worlds; an object does not): `{v: 1, id, op, ...}`, with an op of the list, a random id (`crypto.getRandomValues`: `randomUUID` needs a secure context, which an https form inside an http page is not), a label of at most 300 code points and no other key, read strictly on both sides. The bridge acts on the event's composed path's first element (inside an open shadow root too), an element of its own document, acknowledges the command with `preventDefault()` and answers it with `tailorit:bridge-result` on the same element, `{v: 1, id, ok, reason}`, both inside the dispatch. A command nobody acknowledged (no bridge in the frame), and one acknowledged but unanswered when the dispatch returns (something on the page took it), get no answer at once: nothing is waited for. Nothing there can end a run: any throw is no answer.
  - **The ops:** `react-select.choose {label, multi}`, react-select's own `selectOption` for the one option whose label, as react-select reads it (its `getOptionLabel`), is exactly the label sent (white space collapsed; groups flattened; none or two, a disabled option or select, the wrong kind, and an option chosen already are refused); and `react.field`, the nearest React component's field (`props.field`, else `props.fieldEntry.field`) as `{type, path}`, two printable strings of at most 100 characters, for Ashby's adapter (Phase 2).
  - **What crosses:** into the page's world, the op, whether the select is a multi-select, a random id and the chosen option's clean label as the frame read it from the page, which reveals the answer as the fill will, a moment earlier; never a profile value. Back, a reason from a closed list and those two strings. A forged command can only make the bridge choose a page's own widget's own option; a forged answer is never trusted: the content script reads the widget back itself and falls back to its events (Filler, "React first"). After the first command nothing answered, or the first choice it claimed that never read back, the run's bridge is off (`BridgeRun`).
- **Messages** (`src/messaging`; the full catalogue goes in `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`):
  - **Envelope.** Every message is `{v: 1, type, …}`, parsed by a strict zod union per channel, with every string and array capped. Every answer is `{ok: true, data}` or `{ok: false, error}`, where `error` is a closed list.
  - **The worker checks each sender:**
    - content messages need our own extension id, a tab and a frame, an ATS address and an active document;
    - page messages must come from our own extension pages;
    - external messages need TailorIT's exact origin and the pending pairing `state`.
    - A known type on the wrong channel is refused.
  - **Content to worker:**
    - `frame/hello` and `run/begin`, which answers a run id and the settings, never the profile;
    - `plan/request`: the frame's whole scan as `FieldSpec[]` (at most 500), the keys this pass plans (`planKeys`, a subset of the specs' keys), and the page's canaries (at most 32) and notices. It answers `PlannedWrite[]`, value-free rows and the keys waiting for the LLM, for `planKeys` only;
    - `plan/poll`, which returns those keys' writes once the AI task is done;
    - `runstate/*`, `report/put` and `policy/hit` (a posting page's value-free pattern id);
    - `panel/open`, from the launcher's trusted click;
    - the `resume` port.
  - **Worker to content:**
    - start, continue (after a pause) and cancel;
    - a policy card's decision, to the one frame that waits at it (since v4 none waits, and the frame answers `expired`);
    - focus a field, confirm a draft;
    - `field/describe`: the labels of a row's suggested options, fetched only when the row is expanded, never stored;
    - `report/update`: the tab's combined report, sent to the frame that shows the in-page panel while it is open;
    - read an answer the user asked to save: it is sent straight to TailorIT and never stored.
  - **External:** `pair/complete {state, code}` only.
  - **The worker's limits:**
    - It calls only `<origin>/api/autofill/*` and `GET <origin>/api/ai/tasks/<uuid>`, with `credentials: 'omit'`, `redirect: 'error'` and `cache: 'no-store'`. It never cancels (`DELETE`s) an AI task: TailorIT gives callers that ask the same one task, and prunes every task itself (the Phase 4 review, T-F2).
    - Every call, the resume included, has `AbortSignal.timeout(25_000)`, because Chrome may stop a worker whose fetch waits longer than 30 s.
    - AI routes answer within 10 s, or return a task to poll every 1.5 s, with a 120 s cap.
    - After a worker restart, `plan/poll` for an unknown run answers `expired`, and the content script re-sends `plan/request` for its waiting fields.
    - A resubmitted batch reattaches through `dedupeKey` while the task is queued or running. A finished one is re-read by the task id kept in RunState (an id, never a value), within its one-hour retention.
  - **Resume transfer:**
    - The content script asks only when a planned write targets a file field.
    - **Before any fetch:** a legacy `.doc` (`fileKind: doc`) is refused: "Your resume is an old Word file. Choose a PDF resume in TailorIT."
    - **What the worker accepts:**
      - files up to 10 MiB, so at most 40 chunks;
      - a PDF whose bytes start with `%PDF-`;
      - a DOCX with the ZIP signature and `X-TailorIT-File-Kind: docx`. It goes only into an input with no `accept`, or one that allows `.docx` or its MIME type; otherwise the field is flagged `pdf-only-input`.
    - **Sending:** a header (name, type, size, SHA-256 checked against `X-TailorIT-Sha256`), then 256 KiB base64 chunks, each acknowledged before the next.
    - **Receiving:** the content script decodes into one buffer, checks size and hash, builds the `File`, and retries once from scratch on any mismatch (then `resume-transfer-failed`). A failed or timed-out fetch flags the field `resume-unavailable`.
    - Both sides release the bytes on completion or within 60 s.
- **Purity is enforced four ways:**
  - `tsconfig.core.json` (lib ES2023, `types: []`): `document`, `URL`, `crypto` and `chrome` cannot even be typed.
  - An ESLint override for `src/core/**` bans impure globals, `Date.now`, `Math.random`, dynamic import, zod, and imports of impure layers, with inline disables refused. Only `import type` from `src/contract/tailorit` is allowed, since it is erased at build.
  - WXT's auto-imports are off.
  - A Vitest test loads every core module in Node with no DOM.
  - Time, randomness, vocabularies and the LLM come in as parameters.
- **Storage:**
  - Content scripts get no storage at all: the platform wrapper hands them none.
  - The worker also calls `storage.local.setAccessLevel({accessLevel: 'TRUSTED_CONTEXTS'})` in `onInstalled` and at every start, before it handles any content message. This needs Chrome 140+, hence the 144 floor. A rejection blocks filling and is shown on the options page.

  | Data | Where | How long |
  |---|---|---|
  | The bearer token | the extension origin's IndexedDB (a content script cannot open it), plus the worker's memory | until disconnect, expiry or a 401 |
  | The profile | the worker's memory | the run; dropped after 5 idle minutes. The sensitive part (authorization, EEO, age, pronouns, pay) is fetched only when the worker meets such a question |
  | Saved answers | the worker's memory, fetched with the profile | the run |
  | Resume bytes, and (Phase G) a cover letter's | the worker, then the content script | seconds |
  | The run's cover letter (Phase G): TailorIT's id of it, where it stands, whether its AI wrote it (TailorIT's word, or written during the run), and the lookup's job id it was asked with | the worker's memory | until the run is replaced or forgotten, the token changes, or the worker stops |
  | Settings | `storage.local` | until changed |
  | RunState (no values) | `storage.local`, indexed per tab in `storage.session` | 24 hours after its last update; at most 20 kept, each at most 256 KiB (`MAX_RUN_STATE_BYTES`). Its records hold at most `RUN_STATE_RECORD_CAPS` entries |
  | Reports (no values) | `storage.session` | until the tab closes, the document that sent it is gone (its frame loads another, or the worker finds it gone), or the browser restarts |
  | Registered frames (origin, platform, document id, flags; never an address) | `storage.session`, `frames:<tabId>:<frameId>` | until the tab closes, the worker finds the frame's document gone (`frame/ping`), or the browser restarts |
  | Runs (no values): tab, frame, document id, application key, dry run, and what became of the run's one sensitive fetch | `storage.session`, `runs:<runId>` | until the tab closes, the frame begins another run, the document it was begun in is gone, or the browser restarts; at most 50 kept |
  | Policy hits from a posting page: pattern ids, and whether a posting page was read and clear (`{hits, clear}`) | `storage.session`, `policy:<applicationKey>`, their order (keys only) in `policy-order` | until the browser restarts; at most 200 applications, the least recently recorded or read going first |
  | The pending pairing: a single-use state of 32 random bytes | `storage.session`, `pairing` | 10 minutes, or its first use |
  | The account's name and email, from TailorIT's session | the worker's memory | until the token changes or the worker stops |
  | Each run's lookup answer: the job, its stored posting text (read only by the policy patterns), the suggested resume | the worker's memory | until the run is replaced or forgotten, or the worker stops |
  | Accepted matches (sealed: the key's value part and the chosen option's normalized label as HMAC-SHA-256 under a secret of the browser session's, kept beside them; the v3 Phase 7a review) | `storage.session`, in memory, never on disk. Never unconfirmed LLM picks; never EEO, legal, consent or sensitive fields | until the browser restarts |
  | Anonymous counters | `storage.local`, only when opted in | until cleared |

## Message catalogue
Every shape is a strict zod schema in `src/messaging/schemas.ts`; this section says who sends each message, what the worker does with it, and what it answers. Built in Phase 2 (`src/background`).

**The envelope.**
- A message is `{v: 1, type, …}`. An answer is `{ok: true, data}` or `{ok: false, error, retryAfterSeconds?}`, where `error` is one of `bad_message`, `bad_sender`, `not_connected`, `not_implemented`, `unavailable`, `rate_limited`, `too_large`, `expired`, `internal` and (the v4 review) `not_found` ("v4", `signin/credential` refusals).
- In every channel: an unknown type, an extra field or a value past its cap is `bad_message`; a known type on the wrong channel is `bad_sender`; a handler that throws is `internal`.
